"""РћРєРЅРѕ РІРёР·СѓР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёРё РіСЂР°С„Р° СЃРІСЏР·РµР№ (РњРµРіР°-РІРµСЂСЃРёСЏ)."""
import math
import traceback
from datetime import datetime as dt
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QGraphicsView, 
    QGraphicsScene, QGraphicsEllipseItem, QGraphicsTextItem, 
    QPushButton, QLabel, QGraphicsItem, QGraphicsPathItem, 
    QTextBrowser, QSplitter, QWidget, QSlider, QCheckBox
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from PySide6.QtGui import QPen, QColor, QBrush, QFont, QPainter, QPainterPath

# РџРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РґРёР°Р»РѕРі РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРё С…РѕСЃС‚Р°
try:
    from paradox_gui.dialogs.host_story_dialog import HostStoryDialog
    from paradox.analyzers.story import build_host_story
    HAS_STORY = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_STORY = False


class ZoomableView(QGraphicsView):
    def __init__(self, scene, parent=None):
        super().__init__(scene, parent)
        self._scene = scene
        self.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)
        self.setDragMode(QGraphicsView.DragMode.ScrollHandDrag)
        self.setTransformationAnchor(QGraphicsView.ViewportAnchor.AnchorUnderMouse)
        self.setResizeAnchor(QGraphicsView.ViewportAnchor.AnchorUnderMouse)
        self.setBackgroundBrush(QColor("#1e1e24"))

    def wheelEvent(self, event):
        zoom_factor = 1.15 if event.angleDelta().y() > 0 else 1 / 1.15
        self.scale(zoom_factor, zoom_factor)

    def fit_in_view(self):
        rect = self._scene.itemsBoundingRect()
        if not rect.isNull() and rect.width() > 0 and rect.height() > 0:
            self.fitInView(rect, Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatio)


class InteractiveNode(QGraphicsEllipseItem):
    def __init__(self, x, y, r, ip, roles, total_bytes, dialog):
        super().__init__(x - r, y - r, r * 2, r * 2)
        self.ip = ip
        self.dialog = dialog
        roles = roles or []
        total_bytes = total_bytes or 0

        if ip == "Р”СЂСѓРіРёРµ РІРЅРµС€РЅРёРµ" or "/" in ip:
            color = QColor("#555555")
        elif "pivot" in roles or "attacker_candidate" in roles:
            color = QColor("#ff4444")
        elif "victim_candidate" in roles:
            color = QColor("#ffaa00")
        elif x < 300:
            color = QColor("#44aaff")
        else:
            color = QColor("#888888")

        self.default_brush = QBrush(color)
        self.setBrush(self.default_brush)
        self.setPen(QPen(QColor("#ffffff"), 1.5))

        roles_str = ", ".join(roles) if roles else "вЂ”"
        self.setToolTip(f"<b>IP:</b> {ip}<br><b>Р РѕР»СЊ:</b> {roles_str}<br><b>РўСЂР°С„РёРє:</b> {total_bytes:,} Р±Р°Р№С‚")
        self.setFlag(QGraphicsItem.GraphicsItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable, True)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        self.dialog.highlight_node(self.ip)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseDoubleClickEvent(self, event):
        if self.ip not in ("Р”СЂСѓРіРёРµ РІРЅРµС€РЅРёРµ",) and "/" not in self.ip and HAS_STORY:
            self.dialog.open_host_story(self.ip)
        super().mouseDoubleClickEvent(event)


class InteractiveEdge(QGraphicsPathItem):
    def __init__(self, x1, y1, x2, y2, bytes_count, proto, port, src_ip, dst_ip, index, ts=None):
        super().__init__()
        self.src_ip = src_ip
        self.dst_ip = dst_ip
        self.ts = ts or 0.0
        bytes_count = bytes_count or 0

        path = QPainterPath()
        path.moveTo(x1, y1)
        offset = (index % 5) * 20 - 40
        cx = (x1 + x2) / 2
        cy = (y1 + y2) / 2 - 50 + offset
        path.quadTo(cx, cy, x2, y2)
        self.setPath(path)

        pen_width = max(1.0, min(8.0, math.log10(bytes_count + 10) - 1))
        self.default_pen = QPen(QColor("#444455"), pen_width)
        self.highlight_pen = QPen(QColor("#ffffff"), pen_width + 2)

        if "Finding" in str(proto):
            self.default_pen = QPen(QColor("#ff4444"), 2, Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)
            self.highlight_pen = QPen(QColor("#ff8888"), 3, Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)

        self.setPen(self.default_pen)

        port_str = f":{port}" if port else ""
        self.setToolTip(f"<b>РџСЂРѕС‚РѕРєРѕР»:</b> {proto}{port_str}<br><b>Р‘Р°Р№С‚:</b> {bytes_count:,}")


class GraphDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, report: dict, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("Р“СЂР°С„ СЃРІСЏР·РµР№ (Pro)")
        self.resize(1300, 850)
        self.report = report or {}

        self.nodes = {}
        self.edges = []
        self.edges_by_ip = {}
        self.node_times = {}

        self.t_min = 0.0
        self.t_max = 0.0

        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)

        left_widget = QWidget()
        left_layout = QVBoxLayout(left_widget)
        left_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        # РџР°РЅРµР»СЊ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРІ
        filter_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.cb_only_findings = QCheckBox("РўРѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃСЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєРё (Findings)")
        self.cb_only_findings.stateChanged.connect(self._redraw_graph)
        filter_layout.addWidget(self.cb_only_findings)

        self.cb_hide_isolated = QCheckBox("РЎРєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РёР·РѕР»РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СѓР·Р»С‹")
        self.cb_hide_isolated.stateChanged.connect(self._redraw_graph)
        filter_layout.addWidget(self.cb_hide_isolated)

        filter_layout.addStretch()
        left_layout.addLayout(filter_layout)

        self.scene = QGraphicsScene(self)
        self.view = ZoomableView(self.scene)
        left_layout.addWidget(self.view)

        # РџР°РЅРµР»СЊ РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРё
        time_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.lbl_time = QLabel("Р’СЂРµРјСЏ: вЂ”")
        self.lbl_time.setStyleSheet("color: #44aaff; font-weight: bold;")
        time_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_time)

        self.slider = QSlider(Qt.Orientation.Horizontal)
        self.slider.setRange(0, 100)
        self.slider.setValue(100)
        self.slider.valueChanged.connect(self._on_time_changed)
        time_layout.addWidget(self.slider)

        left_layout.addLayout(time_layout)

        # РџСЂР°РІР°СЏ РїР°РЅРµР»СЊ
        right_widget = QWidget()
        right_widget.setFixedWidth(350)
        right_layout = QVBoxLayout(right_widget)
        right_layout.setContentsMargins(10, 0, 0, 0)

        self.info_panel = QTextBrowser()
        self.info_panel.setStyleSheet("background: #2a2a35; border: 1px solid #444; border-radius: 4px;")
        self.info_panel.setHtml("<p style='color:#888;'>РљР»РёРєРЅРёС‚Рµ РЅР° СѓР·РµР», С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ СѓРІРёРґРµС‚СЊ С…СЂРѕРЅРѕР»РѕРіРёСЋ.<br>Р”РІРѕР№РЅРѕР№ РєР»РёРє вЂ” РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ С…РѕСЃС‚Р°.</p>")
        right_layout.addWidget(self.info_panel)

        legend = QLabel(
            "<b>Р›РµРіРµРЅРґР°:</b><br>"
            "<span style='color:#ff4444;'>в—Џ</span> РђС‚Р°РєСѓСЋС‰РёР№ / Pivot<br>"
            "<span style='color:#ffaa00;'>в—Џ</span> Р–РµСЂС‚РІР°<br>"
            "<span style='color:#44aaff;'>в—Џ</span> Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№<br>"
            "<span style='color:#888888;'>в—Џ</span> Р’РЅРµС€РЅРёР№<br>"
            "<span style='color:#555555;'>в—Џ</span> РџРѕРґСЃРµС‚СЊ / Р”СЂСѓРіРёРµ<br>"
            "<span style='color:#ff4444;'>- - -</span> Finding (РЎСЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєР°)<br>"
        )
        legend.setStyleSheet("color: #a0a0b0; font-size: 11px; margin-top: 10px;")
        right_layout.addWidget(legend)

        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_reset = QPushButton("РЎР±СЂРѕСЃ")
        btn_reset.clicked.connect(self.reset_highlight)
        btn_close = QPushButton("Р—Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ")
        btn_close.setObjectName("primary")
        btn_close.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btn_row.addWidget(btn_reset)
        btn_row.addWidget(btn_close)
        right_layout.addLayout(btn_row)

        splitter = QSplitter(Qt.Orientation.Horizontal)
        splitter.addWidget(left_widget)
        splitter.addWidget(right_widget)
        splitter.setSizes([950, 350])
        layout.addWidget(splitter)

        self._draw_graph()
        self.view.fit_in_view()

    def _redraw_graph(self):
        """РџРµСЂРµСЂРёСЃРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РіСЂР°С„ СЃ СѓС‡РµС‚РѕРј С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРІ."""
        self.scene.clear()
        self.nodes.clear()
        self.edges.clear()
        self.edges_by_ip.clear()
        self.node_times.clear()
        self._draw_graph()
        self._on_time_changed(self.slider.value())

    def _get_subnet(self, ip: str) -> str:
        """Р’РµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ /24 РїРѕРґСЃРµС‚СЊ РґР»СЏ IPv4, РёР»Рё СЃР°Рј IP РґР»СЏ IPv6."""
        if ":" in ip: return ip
        parts = ip.split('.')
        if len(parts) == 4:
            return f"{parts[0]}.{parts[1]}.{parts[2]}.0/24"
        return ip

    def _draw_graph(self):
        try:
            only_findings = self.cb_only_findings.isChecked()
            hide_isolated = self.cb_hide_isolated.isChecked()

            entities = self.report.get("entities") or {}
            internal = entities.get("internal") or []
            external = entities.get("external") or []
            flows_dict = self.report.get("flows") or {}
            flows = flows_dict.get("top_by_bytes") or []
            findings = self.report.get("findings") or []

            times = [f.get("first_seen") for f in findings if f.get("first_seen")]
            self.t_min = min(times) if times else 0.0
            self.t_max = max(times) if times else 1.0
            if self.t_min == self.t_max: self.t_max += 1.0

            # РЎР±РѕСЂ С‚СЂР°С„РёРєР°
            traffic_map = {}
            for f in flows:
                src, dst = f.get("src_ip"), f.get("dst_ip")
                b = f.get("bytes") or 0
                if src: traffic_map[src] = traffic_map.get(src, 0) + b
                if dst: traffic_map[dst] = traffic_map.get(dst, 0) + b

            finding_ips = set()
            for f in findings:
                if f.get("src_ip"): finding_ips.add(f.get("src_ip"))
                if f.get("dst_ip"): finding_ips.add(f.get("dst_ip"))

            # РљР»Р°СЃС‚РµСЂРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ РІРЅРµС€РЅРёС… IP РїРѕ /24
            external_clustered = {}
            for e in external:
                ip = e.get("ip")
                if not ip: continue
                subnet = self._get_subnet(ip)
                if subnet not in external_clustered:
                    external_clustered[subnet] = {"roles": [], "bytes": 0, "ips": []}
                external_clustered[subnet]["roles"].extend(e.get("roles") or [])
                external_clustered[subnet]["bytes"] += traffic_map.get(ip, 0)
                external_clustered[subnet]["ips"].append(ip)

            # РџСЂРµРѕР±СЂР°Р·СѓРµРј РІ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє РґР»СЏ СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІРєРё
            ext_nodes = []
            for subnet, data in external_clustered.items():
                ext_nodes.append({
                    "ip": subnet, 
                    "roles": list(set(data["roles"])), 
                    "bytes": data["bytes"],
                    "is_cluster": len(data["ips"]) > 1
                })
            ext_nodes.sort(key=lambda x: x["bytes"], reverse=True)

            MAX_EXTERNAL = 40
            ext_nodes = ext_nodes[:MAX_EXTERNAL]

            internal.sort(key=lambda e: traffic_map.get(e.get("ip"), 0), reverse=True)
            node_data = {}

            # Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРµ
            for i, e in enumerate(internal):
                ip = e.get("ip")
                if not ip: continue
                total_b = traffic_map.get(ip, 0)
                r = min(35, max(15, 15 + math.log10(total_b + 10) * 2))
                if ip in finding_ips: r = max(r, 22) 
                y = (i - len(internal)/2) * 120 + 500
                node_data[ip] = {"x": 150, "y": y, "r": r, "roles": e.get("roles") or [], "bytes": total_b}

            # Р’РЅРµС€РЅРёРµ (РєР»Р°СЃС‚РµСЂС‹)
            cols = 5
            for i, e in enumerate(ext_nodes):
                ip = e["ip"]
                total_b = e["bytes"]
                r = min(30, max(12, 12 + math.log10(total_b + 10) * 2))
                if any(x in finding_ips for x in [ip]): r = max(r, 18)

                col = i % cols
                row = i // cols
                x = 550 + col * 130
                y = 200 + row * 90

                node_data[ip] = {"x": x, "y": y, "r": r, "roles": e["roles"], "bytes": total_b, "is_cluster": e["is_cluster"]}

            # Р РµР±СЂР°
            active_edges_count = 0
            for idx, f in enumerate(flows):
                src, dst = f.get("src_ip"), f.get("dst_ip")
                if not src or not dst: continue

                # РњР°РїРёРј dst_ip РЅР° РїРѕРґСЃРµС‚СЊ, РµСЃР»Рё РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ
                dst_node = self._get_subnet(dst) if dst in [x["ip"] for x in external] else dst

                if src in node_data and dst_node in node_data:
                    n1, n2 = node_data[src], node_data[dst_node]
                    port = f.get("dst_port")
                    edge_ts = max(self.node_times.get(src, self.t_min), self.node_times.get(dst_node, self.t_min))
                    edge = InteractiveEdge(n1["x"], n1["y"], n2["x"], n2["y"], f.get("bytes"), f.get("proto", "?"), port, src, dst_node, idx, ts=edge_ts)
                    self.scene.addItem(edge)
                    self.edges.append(edge)
                    self.edges_by_ip.setdefault(src, []).append(edge)
                    self.edges_by_ip.setdefault(dst_node, []).append(edge)
                    active_edges_count += 1

            # Р РµР±СЂР° РёР· Findings
            for idx, f in enumerate(findings):
                src, dst = f.get("src_ip"), f.get("dst_ip")
                if not src or not dst: continue

                dst_node = self._get_subnet(dst) if dst in [x["ip"] for x in external] else dst

                if src in node_data and dst_node in node_data and src != dst_node:
                    n1, n2 = node_data[src], node_data[dst_node]
                    edge = InteractiveEdge(n1["x"], n1["y"], n2["x"], n2["y"], 10, f"Finding: {f.get('type')}", None, src, dst_node, idx, ts=f.get("first_seen"))
                    self.scene.addItem(edge)
                    self.edges.append(edge)
                    self.edges_by_ip.setdefault(src, []).append(edge)
                    self.edges_by_ip.setdefault(dst_node, []).append(edge)

            # Р’СЂРµРјСЏ РїРѕСЏРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ СѓР·Р»РѕРІ (РјРёРЅ. РІСЂРµРјСЏ СЃСЂРµРґРё РµРіРѕ РЅР°С…РѕРґРѕРє)
            for ip in node_data:
                ip_findings = [f.get("first_seen") for f in findings if f.get("src_ip") == ip or f.get("dst_ip") == ip]
                self.node_times[ip] = min(ip_findings) if ip_findings else self.t_min

            # РћС‚СЂРёСЃРѕРІРєР° СѓР·Р»РѕРІ
            for ip, data in node_data.items():
                # Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ РёР·РѕР»РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹С…
                if hide_isolated and ip not in self.edges_by_ip:
                    continue

                node = InteractiveNode(data["x"], data["y"], data["r"], ip, data["roles"], data["bytes"], self)
                self.scene.addItem(node)
                self.nodes[ip] = node

                # РўРµРєСЃС‚
                display_ip = ip
                if data.get("is_cluster") and "/" in ip:
                    display_ip = ip.split('/')[0] + ".x"

                text = QGraphicsTextItem(display_ip)
                text.setDefaultTextColor(QColor("#ffffff"))
                text.setFont(QFont("Consolas", 9))
                rect = text.boundingRect()
                text.setPos(data["x"] - rect.width()/2, data["y"] + data["r"] + 5)
                self.scene.addItem(text)

            self.scene.setSceneRect(self.scene.itemsBoundingRect())
        except Exception as e:
            err = traceback.format_exc()
            self.info_panel.setHtml(f"<h3 style='color:red;'>РћС€РёР±РєР° РѕС‚СЂРёСЃРѕРІРєРё РіСЂР°С„Р°</h3><pre>{err}</pre>")

    def open_host_story(self, ip: str):
        """РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РѕРєРЅРѕ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРё С…РѕСЃС‚Р° РїСЂРё РґРІРѕР№РЅРѕРј РєР»РёРєРµ."""
        if not HAS_STORY:
            return
        story = build_host_story(self.report, ip)
        dialog = HostStoryDialog(ip, story, self)
        dialog.exec()

    def _on_time_changed(self, value: int):
        current_t = self.t_min + (self.t_max - self.t_min) * (value / 100.0)
        try:
            time_str = dt.fromtimestamp(current_t).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        except:
            time_str = f"{current_t:.1f}"
        self.lbl_time.setText(f"Р’СЂРµРјСЏ: {time_str}")

        for ip, node in self.nodes.items():
            node_t = self.node_times.get(ip, self.t_min)
            node.setOpacity(0.05 if node_t > current_t else 1.0)

        for edge in self.edges:
            edge.setOpacity(0.05 if edge.ts > current_t else 1.0)

    def highlight_node(self, ip: str):
        self._on_time_changed(self.slider.value())

        for edge in self.edges:
            edge.setPen(edge.default_pen)
            if edge.src_ip != ip and edge.dst_ip != ip:
                edge.setOpacity(0.05)

        for n_ip, node in self.nodes.items():
            if n_ip != ip:
                node.setOpacity(0.1)

        if ip in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[ip].setOpacity(1.0)
            self.nodes[ip].setBrush(QBrush(QColor("#ffffff")))

        findings = self.report.get("findings") or []
        ip_findings = [f for f in findings if f.get("src_ip") == ip or f.get("dst_ip") == ip]
        ip_findings.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("first_seen", 0))

        html = f"<h3>{ip}</h3>"

        if ip in self.edges_by_ip:
            for edge in self.edges_by_ip[ip]:
                neighbor = edge.dst_ip if edge.src_ip == ip else edge.src_ip
                edge.setPen(edge.highlight_pen)
                edge.setOpacity(1.0)
                if neighbor in self.nodes:
                    self.nodes[neighbor].setOpacity(1.0)

        if ip_findings:
            html += "<b>РҐСЂРѕРЅРѕР»РѕРіРёСЏ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№:</b><ul style='line-height: 1.4;'>"
            for f in ip_findings:
                ts = f.get("first_seen")
                t_str = dt.fromtimestamp(ts).strftime("%H:%M:%S") if ts else "??:??"
                html += f"<li><span style='color:#ffaa00;'>[{t_str}]</span> {f.get('type')}</li>"
            html += "</ul>"
        else:
            html += "<p style='color:#888;'>РќРµС‚ СЃСЂР°Р±РѕС‚РѕРє РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ.</p>"

        self.info_panel.setHtml(html)

    def reset_highlight(self):
        self.slider.setValue(100)
        for edge in self.edges:
            edge.setPen(edge.default_pen)
        for node in self.nodes.values():
            node.setBrush(node.default_brush)
        self.info_panel.setHtml("<p style='color:#888;'>РљР»РёРєРЅРёС‚Рµ РЅР° СѓР·РµР», С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ СѓРІРёРґРµС‚СЊ РґРµС‚Р°Р»Рё.</p>")



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox_gui\dialogs\host_story_dialog.py

"""Р”РёР°Р»РѕРіРѕРІРѕРµ РѕРєРЅРѕ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРё С…РѕСЃС‚Р° (С‚Р°Р№РјР»Р°Р№РЅ)."""
from datetime import datetime
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QTextBrowser, QPushButton, QHBoxLayout
)

class HostStoryDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, ip: str, story: dict, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle(f"РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ С…РѕСЃС‚Р°: {ip}")
        self.resize(700, 600)
        self._build_ui(story)

    def _build_ui(self, story: dict):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        browser = QTextBrowser()
        browser.setOpenExternalLinks(False)

        events = story.get("events", [])
        summary = story.get("summary", {})

        html = f"<h2>РҐСЂРѕРЅРѕР»РѕРіРёСЏ РґР»СЏ {story.get('target_ip', '?')}</h2>"
        html += f"<p><b>Р’СЃРµРіРѕ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№:</b> {summary.get('total_events', 0)} "
        html += f"| <b>РќР°С…РѕРґРѕРє:</b> {summary.get('findings_count', 0)} "
        html += f"| <b>РџРѕС‚РѕРєРѕРІ:</b> {summary.get('flows_count', 0)}</p><hr>"

        if not events:
            html += "<p style='color:#a0a0b0;'>РЎРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№ РґР»СЏ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ IP РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ.</p>"
        else:
            html += "<ul style='line-height: 1.6;'>"
            for ev in events:
                ts = ev.get("ts")
                time_str = "??:??:??"
                if ts:
                    try:
                        time_str = datetime.fromtimestamp(float(ts)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
                    except (OSError, ValueError):
                        time_str = str(ts)

                if ev["type"] == "flow":
                    d = ev["data"]
                    src = f"{d.get('src_ip')}:{d.get('src_port', '')}"
                    dst = f"{d.get('dst_ip')}:{d.get('dst_port', '')}"
                    byt = d.get('bytes', 0)
                    html += f"<li><b>[{time_str}]</b> РџРѕС‚РѕРє: {src} &rarr; {dst} ({byt} Р±Р°Р№С‚)</li>"
                elif ev["type"] == "finding":
                    d = ev["data"]
                    ftype = d.get('type', '?')
                    val = d.get('value', '')
                    html += f"<li><b>[{time_str}]</b> <span style='color:#ff7777;'>РЎСЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєР°: {ftype} ({val})</span></li>"
            html += "</ul>"

        browser.setHtml(html)
        layout.addWidget(browser)

        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_row.addStretch()
        btn_close = QPushButton("Р—Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ")
        btn_close.setObjectName("primary")
        btn_close.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btn_row.addWidget(btn_close)
        layout.addLayout(btn_row)

# FILE: \paradox_gui\dialogs\what_if_dialog.py

"""Р”РёР°Р»РѕРі РґР»СЏ What-If Р°РЅР°Р»РёР·Р°."""
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QPlainTextEdit, QPushButton
)
import re

class WhatIfDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("What-If (РСЃРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ)")
        self.resize(400, 300)
        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        layout.addWidget(QLabel("РСЃРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ IP-Р°РґСЂРµСЃР° (С‡РµСЂРµР· Р·Р°РїСЏС‚СѓСЋ РёР»Рё СЃ РЅРѕРІРѕР№ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё):"))
        self.te_ips = QPlainTextEdit()
        self.te_ips.setPlaceholderText("10.0.0.5, 192.168.1.1")
        layout.addWidget(self.te_ips)

        layout.addWidget(QLabel("РСЃРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ РґРѕРјРµРЅС‹ (С‡Р°СЃС‚РёС‡РЅРѕРµ СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёРµ):"))
        self.te_domains = QPlainTextEdit()
        self.te_domains.setPlaceholderText("bad.com, example.org")
        layout.addWidget(self.te_domains)

        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_row.addStretch()
        btn_cancel = QPushButton("РћС‚РјРµРЅР°")
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btn_apply = QPushButton("РџСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ")
        btn_apply.setObjectName("primary")
        btn_apply.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btn_row.addWidget(btn_cancel)
        btn_row.addWidget(btn_apply)
        layout.addLayout(btn_row)

    def get_exclusions(self) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
        ips_text = self.te_ips.toPlainText().strip()
        domains_text = self.te_domains.toPlainText().strip()

        ips = [i.strip() for i in re.split(r'[\s,]+', ips_text) if i.strip()]
        domains = [d.strip() for d in re.split(r'[\s,]+', domains_text) if d.strip()]

        return ips, domains

# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox_gui\views\enrich_view.py
# ==========================================
"""Р’РєР»Р°РґРєР° В«РћР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёРµВ» вЂ” РІС‹Р±РѕСЂ IOC Рё Threat Intel РїРѕ РЅРёРј.

РЎС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂР°:
    Р’РµСЂС…РЅСЏСЏ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Р° вЂ” РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚С‹ IOC РёР· findings. РћРґРЅР° СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° = IOC.
    Р§РµРєР±РѕРєСЃС‹, РєРЅРѕРїРєРё В«Р’С‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ РІСЃРµ / РЎРЅСЏС‚СЊ РІСЃРµ / РћР±РѕРіР°С‚РёС‚СЊ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅС‹РµВ».

    РќРёР¶РЅСЏСЏ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Р° вЂ” СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹ РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ. РћРґРЅР° СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° = РїР°СЂР°
    (IOC Г— РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂ). Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РїРѕ РјРµСЂРµ РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ, СЂР°Р·РЅС‹РјРё РїР°СЂС‚РёСЏРјРё.

РџРѕС‡РµРјСѓ РґРІРµ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹, Р° РЅРµ РѕРґРЅР°:
    РЎРїРёСЃРѕРє РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚РѕРІ РЅСѓР¶РµРЅ Р”Рћ РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ (С‡С‚Рѕ РІС‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ). Р РµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹
    РёРјРµСЋС‚ РґСЂСѓРіРѕР№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ (РїРѕ РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂСѓ РЅР° СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ), РїРѕС‚РѕРјСѓ С‡С‚Рѕ Сѓ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ
    IOC РјРѕР¶РµС‚ Р±С‹С‚СЊ 2вЂ“3 РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРєР° СЃ СЂР°Р·РЅРѕР№ С„РѕСЂРјРѕР№ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…. РЎРјРµС€РёРІР°С‚СЊ
    РІ РѕРґРЅСѓ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Сѓ вЂ” РїРѕС‚РµСЂСЏРµРј Р»РёР±Рѕ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂ, Р»РёР±Рѕ СЂР°Р·РІС‘СЂРЅСѓС‚РѕСЃС‚СЊ.

РљР»СЋС‡Рё API:
    РЇРґСЂРѕ С‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚ ABUSEIPDB_API_KEY РёР· os.environ РІ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР°.
    РљР»СЋС‡ РІС‹СЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РІ РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°С… в†’ Threat Intel.
"""
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from PySide6.QtGui import QBrush, QColor
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QHBoxLayout,
    QLabel,
    QMessageBox,
    QProgressBar,
    QPushButton,
    QSplitter,
    QTableWidget,
    QTableWidgetItem,
    QVBoxLayout,
    QWidget,
)

from paradox_gui.workers.enrichment_worker import EnrichmentWorker


# РРЅРґРµРєСЃС‹ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРѕРє РІРµСЂС…РЅРµР№ (РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚С‹) Рё РЅРёР¶РЅРµР№ (СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹) С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†.
# РњР°РіРёС‡РµСЃРєРёРµ С‡РёСЃР»Р° РІ РєРѕРґРµ С‡РёС‚Р°РµРјС‹, РЅРѕ РёРјРµРЅРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРЅСЃС‚Р°РЅС‚С‹ вЂ” РЅР°РґС‘Р¶РЅРµРµ.
C_CHECK, C_IOC, C_TYPE, C_STATUS = range(4)
R_IOC, R_TYPE, R_PROVIDER, R_DATA = range(4)


class EnrichView(QWidget):
    """Р’РєР»Р°РґРєР° В«РћР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёРµВ» СЃ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂРѕРј IOC Рё СЂР°Р·РІС‘СЂРЅСѓС‚С‹РјРё СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚Р°РјРё."""

    # РЎРёРіРЅР°Р» В«РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёРµ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РµРЅРѕВ». Р­РјРёС‚РёС‚СЃСЏ РѕРґРёРЅ СЂР°Р·
    # РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РєР°Р¶РґРѕР№ РїР°СЂС‚РёРё. РќСѓР¶РµРЅ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ main_window РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёР»
    # Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјС‹Рµ РІРєР»Р°РґРєРё (РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ) Р±РµР· РїСЂСЏРјРѕРіРѕ Р·РЅР°РЅРёСЏ РїСЂРѕ РІРѕСЂРєРµСЂ.
    enrichment_finished = Signal()

    def __init__(self, parent=None) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._report: dict | None = None
        self._config_overrides: dict = {}
        self._worker: EnrichmentWorker | None = None
        # [(type, ioc), ...] вЂ” РІСЃРµ РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚С‹ РёР· РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРµРіРѕ report.
        self._candidates: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        # {(type, ioc): {source: data}} вЂ” РЅР°РєРѕРїР»РµРЅРЅС‹Рµ СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹.
        # РњРµСЂРґР¶РёС‚СЃСЏ РјРµР¶РґСѓ РїР°СЂС‚РёСЏРјРё РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ.
        self._enrichment: dict = {}
        self._build_ui()

    # =========================================================================
    # UI
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        outer = QVBoxLayout(self)
        outer.setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24)
        outer.setSpacing(10)

        title = QLabel("РћР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёРµ")
        title.setObjectName("h1")
        outer.addWidget(title)

        subtitle = QLabel(
            "Threat Intel РїРѕ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅС‹Рј IOC. Shodan InternetDB вЂ” Р±РµР· РєР»СЋС‡Р°, "
            "AbuseIPDB вЂ” РЅСѓР¶РµРЅ РєР»СЋС‡ (РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё в†’ Threat Intel). "
            "Р”РІРѕР№РЅРѕР№ РєР»РёРє РїРѕ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРµ вЂ” РїРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ JSON."
        )
        subtitle.setObjectName("muted")
        subtitle.setWordWrap(True)
        outer.addWidget(subtitle)

        # --- РџР°РЅРµР»СЊ СѓРїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ ---
        controls = QHBoxLayout()
        controls.setSpacing(8)

        self._status = QLabel("РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° РїСЂРѕРіРѕРЅРёС‚Рµ pcap РІРѕ РІРєР»Р°РґРєРµ В«РџСЂРѕРіРѕРЅВ».")
        self._status.setObjectName("muted")
        controls.addWidget(self._status, stretch=1)

        self._select_all_btn = QPushButton("Р’С‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ РІСЃРµ")
        self._select_all_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self._set_all(True))
        self._select_all_btn.setEnabled(False)
        controls.addWidget(self._select_all_btn)

        self._select_none_btn = QPushButton("РЎРЅСЏС‚СЊ РІСЃРµ")
        self._select_none_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self._set_all(False))
        self._select_none_btn.setEnabled(False)
        controls.addWidget(self._select_none_btn)

        self._progress = QProgressBar()
        self._progress.setRange(0, 0)
        self._progress.setTextVisible(False)
        self._progress.setVisible(False)
        self._progress.setFixedWidth(140)
        controls.addWidget(self._progress)

        self._enrich_btn = QPushButton("РћР±РѕРіР°С‚РёС‚СЊ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ")
        self._enrich_btn.setObjectName("primary")
        self._enrich_btn.setEnabled(False)
        self._enrich_btn.clicked.connect(self._on_enrich)
        controls.addWidget(self._enrich_btn)

        outer.addLayout(controls)

        # --- Splitter: РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚С‹ СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ, СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹ СЃРЅРёР·Сѓ ---
        splitter = QSplitter(Qt.Orientation.Vertical)
        splitter.setChildrenCollapsible(False)
        splitter.setHandleWidth(6)

        # Р’РµСЂС… вЂ” РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚С‹.
        top = QWidget()
        top_l = QVBoxLayout(top)
        top_l.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        top_l.setSpacing(4)
        top_l.addWidget(_section_label("РљР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚С‹"))

        self._cand_table = QTableWidget()
        self._cand_table.setColumnCount(4)
        self._cand_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(
            ["", "IOC", "РўРёРї", "РЎС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ"]
        )
        self._cand_table.setEditTriggers(
            QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers
        )
        self._cand_table.setSelectionBehavior(
            QTableWidget.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows
        )
        self._cand_table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self._cand_table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self._cand_table.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(True)
        self._cand_table.setColumnWidth(C_CHECK, 30)
        self._cand_table.setColumnWidth(C_IOC, 320)
        self._cand_table.setColumnWidth(C_TYPE, 100)
        self._cand_table.itemChanged.connect(self._on_candidate_changed)
        top_l.addWidget(self._cand_table, stretch=1)
        splitter.addWidget(top)

        # РќРёР· вЂ” СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹.
        bottom = QWidget()
        bottom_l = QVBoxLayout(bottom)
        bottom_l.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        bottom_l.setSpacing(4)
        bottom_l.addWidget(_section_label("Р РµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹"))

        self._res_table = QTableWidget()
        self._res_table.setColumnCount(4)
        self._res_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(
            ["IOC", "РўРёРї", "РџСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂ", "Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ"]
        )
        self._res_table.setEditTriggers(
            QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers
        )
        self._res_table.setSelectionBehavior(
            QTableWidget.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows
        )
        self._res_table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self._res_table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self._res_table.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(True)
        self._res_table.setColumnWidth(R_IOC, 320)
        self._res_table.setColumnWidth(R_TYPE, 100)
        self._res_table.setColumnWidth(R_PROVIDER, 160)
        self._res_table.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self._on_result_double_clicked)
        bottom_l.addWidget(self._res_table, stretch=1)
        splitter.addWidget(bottom)

        # РџСЂРёРѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚РЅС‹Рµ РїСЂРѕРїРѕСЂС†РёРё: РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚РѕРІ РІРёРґРёРј СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ РјРЅРѕРіРѕ.
        splitter.setStretchFactor(0, 2)
        splitter.setStretchFactor(1, 3)

        outer.addWidget(splitter, stretch=1)

        self._stats_label = QLabel("")
        self._stats_label.setObjectName("muted")
        outer.addWidget(self._stats_label)

    # =========================================================================
    # РџРЈР‘Р›РР§РќРћР• API
    # =========================================================================

    def set_report(self, report: dict | None) -> None:
        """РџСЂРёРЅСЏС‚СЊ РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ report РёР· RunView. РЎР±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°РµС‚ РЅР°РєРѕРїР»РµРЅРЅРѕРµ."""
        self._report = report
        self._enrichment = {}

        if report is None:
            self._candidates = []
            self._refresh_candidates()
            self._refresh_results()
            self._update_status("РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° РїСЂРѕРіРѕРЅРёС‚Рµ pcap РІРѕ РІРєР»Р°РґРєРµ В«РџСЂРѕРіРѕРЅВ».")
            return

        try:
            from paradox.integrations.enricher import (
                collect_iocs_from_findings,
            )
        except ImportError as e:
            self._candidates = []
            self._refresh_candidates()
            self._refresh_results()
            self._update_status(f"РћС€РёР±РєР° РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚Р° СЏРґСЂР°: {e}")
            return

        findings = report.get("findings") or []
        self._candidates = list(collect_iocs_from_findings(findings))
        self._refresh_candidates()
        self._refresh_results()

        if not self._candidates:
            self._update_status(
                "РљР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚РѕРІ РЅРµС‚: РІ findings РЅРµ РЅР°С€Р»РѕСЃСЊ РІРЅРµС€РЅРёС… IP Рё "
                "РґРѕРјРµРЅРѕРІ."
            )
            return

        self._update_status(
            f"Р“РѕС‚РѕРІРѕ Рє РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёСЋ. РљР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚РѕРІ: {len(self._candidates)}."
        )

    def set_config_overrides(self, overrides: dict) -> None:
        """РњРµСЂРґР¶ РїРµСЂРµРѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРёР№ Config РёР· РќР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРє. РљР°Рє РІ RunView."""
        self._config_overrides.update(overrides)

    # =========================================================================
    # HANDLERS
    # =========================================================================

    def _on_enrich(self) -> None:
        if not self._report:
            return
        if self._worker and self._worker.isRunning():
            return

        selected = self._selected_iocs()
        if not selected:
            QMessageBox.information(
                self, "РќРµС‡РµРіРѕ РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰Р°С‚СЊ",
                "РћС‚РјРµС‚СЊС‚Рµ С…РѕС‚СЏ Р±С‹ РѕРґРёРЅ IOC РІ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Рµ РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚РѕРІ.",
            )
            return

        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
            config = Config()
            for k, v in self._config_overrides.items():
                if hasattr(config, k):
                    object.__setattr__(config, k, v)
        except ImportError as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(
                self, "РћС€РёР±РєР°", f"РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ Config: {e}",
            )
            return

        self._set_busy(True)
        self._update_status(f"РћР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёРµ {len(selected)} IOCвЂ¦")

        self._worker = EnrichmentWorker(self._report, config, selected)
        self._worker.finished_ok.connect(self._on_ok)
        self._worker.error.connect(self._on_error)
        self._worker.finished.connect(self._on_done)
        self._worker.start()

    def _on_ok(self, payload: object) -> None:
        data = payload or {}
        stats = data.get("stats") or {}
        new_enrichment = data.get("enrichment") or {}

        # РњРµСЂРґР¶РёРј вЂ” СѓР¶Рµ РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰С‘РЅРЅС‹Рµ IOC РЅРµ С‚РµСЂСЏСЋС‚СЃСЏ.
        self._enrichment.update(new_enrichment)

        # РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РѕР±Рµ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹: СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃС‹ РІ РІРµСЂС…РЅРµР№, СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё РІ РЅРёР¶РЅРµР№.
        self._refresh_candidates()
        self._refresh_results()

        self._update_status(
            f"РћР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРѕ {stats.get('iocs_enriched', 0)} РёР· "
            f"{stats.get('iocs_total', 0)} IOC Р·Р° "
            f"{stats.get('duration_sec', 0):.1f} СЃРµРє."
        )
        self._stats_label.setText(
            f"РљСЌС€: РїРѕРїР°РґР°РЅРёР№ {stats.get('cache_hits', 0)}, "
            f"РїСЂРѕРјР°С…РѕРІ {stats.get('cache_misses', 0)} В· "
            f"Р—Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ: {stats.get('provider_calls', 0)} В· "
            f"РћС€РёР±РѕРє: {stats.get('errors', 0)} В· "
            f"Rate limited: {stats.get('rate_limited', 0)}"
        )

        # РЎРёРіРЅР°Р» РЅР°СЂСѓР¶Сѓ: main_window РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚ В«РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋВ».
        self.enrichment_finished.emit()

    def _on_error(self, msg: str) -> None:
        self._update_status(f"РћС€РёР±РєР°: {msg}")
        QMessageBox.critical(self, "РћС€РёР±РєР° РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРёСЏ", msg)

    def _on_done(self) -> None:
        self._set_busy(False)
        self._worker = None

    def _on_candidate_changed(self, item: QTableWidgetItem) -> None:
        if item.column() != C_CHECK:
            return
        self._update_status(None)  # С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє РїРµСЂРµСЃС‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊ

    def _on_result_double_clicked(self, item: QTableWidgetItem) -> None:
        row = item.row()
        ioc = self._res_table.item(row, R_IOC).text()
        source = self._res_table.item(row, R_PROVIDER).text()
        data = self._find_enrichment(ioc, source)
        if data is None:
            return
        import json
        QMessageBox.information(
            self, f"{source} В· {ioc}",
            f"<pre style='font-family: Consolas, monospace;'>"
            f"{json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)}"
            f"</pre>",
        )

    # =========================================================================
    # Р’Р•Р РҐРќРЇРЇ РўРђР‘Р›РР¦Рђ: РљРђРќР”РР”РђРўР«
    # =========================================================================

    def _refresh_candidates(self) -> None:
        self._cand_table.blockSignals(True)
        try:
            self._cand_table.setRowCount(len(self._candidates))
            for row, key in enumerate(self._candidates):
                self._fill_candidate_row(row, key)
        finally:
            self._cand_table.blockSignals(False)

        has = bool(self._candidates)
        self._select_all_btn.setEnabled(has)
        self._select_none_btn.setEnabled(has)
        self._enrich_btn.setEnabled(
            has and not (self._worker and self._worker.isRunning())
        )

    def _fill_candidate_row(self, row: int, key: tuple[str, str]) -> None:
        ioc_type, ioc = key

        check = QTableWidgetItem()
        check.setFlags(
            Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsUserCheckable | Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsEnabled
        )
        # РџРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ РѕС‚РјРµС‡РµРЅРѕ РІСЃС‘, С‡С‚Рѕ РµС‰С‘ РЅРµ РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРѕ. Р Р°РЅРµРµ
        # РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰С‘РЅРЅС‹Рµ вЂ” СЃРЅСЏС‚С‹, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ РЅРµ РіРѕРЅСЏР» РёС… СЃРЅРѕРІР°.
        already = key in self._enrichment
        check.setCheckState(
            Qt.CheckState.Unchecked if already else Qt.CheckState.Checked
        )
        self._cand_table.setItem(row, C_CHECK, check)

        ioc_item = QTableWidgetItem(ioc)
        ioc_item.setForeground(QBrush(QColor("#e0e0e0")))
        ioc_item.setToolTip(ioc)  # AUTO-FIXED-ENRICH-WIDTH: РїРѕР»РЅРѕРµ РёРјСЏ
        self._cand_table.setItem(row, C_IOC, ioc_item)

        type_item = QTableWidgetItem(ioc_type)
        type_item.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        type_item.setForeground(QBrush(QColor("#a0a0b0")))
        self._cand_table.setItem(row, C_TYPE, type_item)

        if already:
            sources = list(self._enrichment.get(key, {}).keys())
            status_text = f"вњ“ {', '.join(sources)}"
            status_color = "#50fa7b"
        else:
            status_text = "РЅРµ РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰С‘РЅ"
            status_color = "#a0a0b0"

        status_item = QTableWidgetItem(status_text)
        status_item.setForeground(QBrush(QColor(status_color)))
        self._cand_table.setItem(row, C_STATUS, status_item)

    def _set_all(self, checked: bool) -> None:
        state = Qt.CheckState.Checked if checked else Qt.CheckState.Unchecked
        self._cand_table.blockSignals(True)
        try:
            for row in range(self._cand_table.rowCount()):
                item = self._cand_table.item(row, C_CHECK)
                if item is not None:
                    item.setCheckState(state)
        finally:
            self._cand_table.blockSignals(False)
        self._update_status(None)

    def _selected_iocs(self) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
        out: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        for row in range(self._cand_table.rowCount()):
            item = self._cand_table.item(row, C_CHECK)
            if item is not None and item.checkState() == Qt.CheckState.Checked:
                if row < len(self._candidates):
                    out.append(self._candidates[row])
        return out

    # =========================================================================
    # РќРР–РќРЇРЇ РўРђР‘Р›РР¦Рђ: Р Р•Р—РЈР›Р¬РўРђРўР«
    # =========================================================================

    def _refresh_results(self) -> None:
        """Р Р°Р·РІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ self._enrichment РІ РїР»РѕСЃРєРёР№ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє СЃС‚СЂРѕРє."""
        rows: list[tuple[str, str, str, str, dict]] = []
        for (ioc_type, ioc), per_source in self._enrichment.items():
            for source, data in per_source.items():
                rows.append((ioc, ioc_type, source, _summarize(source, data), data))

        # РЎРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІРєР°: СЃРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° IP, РїРѕС‚РѕРј РґРѕРјРµРЅС‹; РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё вЂ” РїРѕ IOC, Р·Р°С‚РµРј
        # РїРѕ РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂСѓ. РЎС‚Р°Р±РёР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє РїРѕРјРѕРіР°РµС‚ РіР»Р°Р·Р°РјРё РёСЃРєР°С‚СЊ.
        rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r[1], r[0], r[2]))

        self._res_table.setRowCount(len(rows))
        for i, (ioc, t, source, summary, _) in enumerate(rows):
            ioc_item = QTableWidgetItem(ioc)
            ioc_item.setForeground(QBrush(QColor("#e0e0e0")))
            ioc_item.setToolTip(ioc)  # AUTO-FIXED-ENRICH-WIDTH: РїРѕР»РЅРѕРµ РёРјСЏ
            self._res_table.setItem(i, R_IOC, ioc_item)

            type_item = QTableWidgetItem(t)
            type_item.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            type_item.setForeground(QBrush(QColor("#a0a0b0")))
            self._res_table.setItem(i, R_TYPE, type_item)

            prov_item = QTableWidgetItem(source)
            prov_item.setForeground(QBrush(QColor("#5b8cff")))
            self._res_table.setItem(i, R_PROVIDER, prov_item)

            data_item = QTableWidgetItem(summary)
            data_item.setToolTip(summary)  # AUTO-FIXED-ENRICH-WIDTH
            self._res_table.setItem(i, R_DATA, data_item)

        if not rows:
            self._stats_label.setText(
                "Р РµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚РѕРІ РїРѕРєР° РЅРµС‚ вЂ” РІС‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ IOC СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ Рё РЅР°Р¶РјРёС‚Рµ "
                "В«РћР±РѕРіР°С‚РёС‚СЊ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅС‹РµВ»."
            )

    def _find_enrichment(self, ioc: str, source: str):
        for (_, val), per_source in self._enrichment.items():
            if val == ioc and source in per_source:
                return per_source[source]
        return None

    # =========================================================================
    # РћР‘Р©Р•Р•
    # =========================================================================

    def _update_status(self, text: str | None) -> None:
        """РћР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃР°. text=None вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїРµСЂРµСЃС‡С‘С‚ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєР°."""
        total = len(self._candidates)
        selected = len(self._selected_iocs()) if total else 0
        already = sum(1 for k in self._candidates if k in self._enrichment)

        counter_parts = []
        if total:
            counter_parts.append(f"Р’С‹Р±СЂР°РЅРѕ: {selected} РёР· {total}")
        if already:
            counter_parts.append(f"РѕР±РѕРіР°С‰РµРЅРѕ: {already}")
        counter = " В· ".join(counter_parts)

        if text is None:
            # РҐРѕС‚РёРј С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє, СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёРІ Р±Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚.
            current = self._status.text()
            # РћС‚СЂРµР¶РµРј СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Р№ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє, РµСЃР»Рё Р±С‹Р».
            base = current.split(" В· Р’С‹Р±СЂР°РЅРѕ:")[0]
            self._status.setText(
                f"{base} В· {counter}" if counter else base
            )
            return

        if counter:
            self._status.setText(f"{text} В· {counter}")
        else:
            self._status.setText(text)

    def _set_busy(self, busy: bool) -> None:
        self._enrich_btn.setEnabled(
            not busy and bool(self._candidates)
        )
        self._select_all_btn.setEnabled(not busy and bool(self._candidates))
        self._select_none_btn.setEnabled(not busy and bool(self._candidates))
        self._progress.setVisible(busy)


# =============================================================================
# РҐР•Р›РџР•Р Р«
# =============================================================================

def _section_label(text: str) -> QLabel:
    """РџРѕРґРїРёСЃСЊ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»Р° РјРµР¶РґСѓ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Р°РјРё: РЅРµР±РѕР»СЊС€РѕР№, РїСЂРёРіР»СѓС€С‘РЅРЅС‹Р№."""
    lbl = QLabel(text)
    lbl.setObjectName("muted")
    lbl.setStyleSheet("font-weight: 600;")
    return lbl


def _summarize(source: str, data) -> str:
    """РљСЂР°С‚РєР°СЏ РІС‹Р¶РёРјРєР° РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р° РїСЂРѕРІР°Р№РґРµСЂР° РґР»СЏ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєРё В«Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹РµВ»."""
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return str(data)[:120]

    if source == "abuseipdb":
        score = data.get("abuseConfidenceScore")
        country = data.get("countryCode")
        isp = data.get("isp", "")
        parts = []
        if score is not None:
            parts.append(f"score={score}")
        if country:
            parts.append(f"country={country}")
        if isp:
            parts.append(f"isp={isp[:40]}")
        return " В· ".join(parts) or "вЂ”"

    if source == "shodan_internetdb":
        ports = data.get("ports") or []
        vulns = data.get("vulns") or []
        tags = data.get("tags") or []
        hostnames = data.get("hostnames") or []
        parts = []
        if ports:
            parts.append(f"ports={ports[:8]}")
        if vulns:
            parts.append(f"vulns={len(vulns)}")
        if tags:
            parts.append(f"tags={tags[:3]}")
        if hostnames:
            parts.append(f"hostnames={hostnames[:2]}")
        return " В· ".join(parts) or "вЂ”"

    bits = []
    for k, v in list(data.items())[:4]:
        bits.append(f"{k}={str(v)[:30]}")
    return " В· ".join(bits) or "вЂ”"

# AUTO-FIXED-ENRICH-WIDTH



# ==========================================
# FILE: \paradox_gui\views\export_view.py

"""Р’РєР»Р°РґРєР° Р­РєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚Р° (CSV Рё STIX) СЃ РєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµРј РІ Р±СѓС„РµСЂ."""
import csv
import json
import html
import uuid
import ipaddress
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton, 
    QTextBrowser, QFileDialog, QComboBox, QMessageBox, QApplication
)

def _is_private_ip(ip_str: str) -> bool:
    try:
        return ipaddress.ip_address(ip_str).is_private
    except ValueError:
        return False

def _extract_stix_iocs(findings: list) -> list[dict]:
    indicators = []
    seen_values = set()

    for f in findings:
        for ip_field in ("src_ip", "dst_ip"):
            ip = f.get(ip_field)
            if ip and not _is_private_ip(ip) and ip not in seen_values:
                seen_values.add(ip)
                indicators.append({
                    "type": "indicator",
                    "spec_version": "2.1",
                    "id": f"indicator--{uuid.uuid4()}",
                    "created": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                    "pattern": f"[ipv4-addr:value = '{ip}']",
                    "pattern_type": "stix",
                    "valid_from": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                    "name": "External IP",
                    "description": f"РќР°Р№РґРµРЅ РІ РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂРµ: {f.get('type')}"
                })

        val = str(f.get("value", ""))
        domain_match = re.search(r'([a-zA-Z0-9-]+\.[a-zA-Z0-9-]+\.[a-zA-Z]{2,})', val)
        if domain_match:
            domain = domain_match.group(1)
            if domain not in seen_values:
                seen_values.add(domain)
                indicators.append({
                    "type": "indicator",
                    "spec_version": "2.1",
                    "id": f"indicator--{uuid.uuid4()}",
                    "created": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                    "pattern": f"[domain-name:value = '{domain}']",
                    "pattern_type": "stix",
                    "valid_from": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                    "name": "Domain",
                    "description": f"РќР°Р№РґРµРЅ РІ РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂРµ: {f.get('type')}"
                })

    return indicators

class ExportView(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._report = None
        self._current_export_data = ""
        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)
        layout.setSpacing(15)

        lbl = QLabel("Р­РєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… (SIEM / Threat Intelligence)")
        lbl.setStyleSheet("font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; color: #e0e0e0;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl)

        info = QLabel("Р’С‹ РјРѕР¶РµС‚Рµ РІС‹РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ Findings Рё IOC РІ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Рµ CSV (РґР»СЏ Excel) РёР»Рё STIX 2.1 (РґР»СЏ SIEM/EDR).")
        info.setStyleSheet("color: #a0a0b0;")
        layout.addWidget(info)

        ctrl_layout = QHBoxLayout()

        self.format_combo = QComboBox()
        self.format_combo.addItem("CSV (Findings)", "csv")
        self.format_combo.addItem("STIX 2.1 (IOC Bundle)", "stix")
        self.format_combo.setMinimumWidth(250)
        self.format_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._update_preview)
        ctrl_layout.addWidget(self.format_combo)

        self.btn_copy = QPushButton("РљРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РІ Р±СѓС„РµСЂ")
        self.btn_copy.clicked.connect(self._on_copy_clicked)
        self.btn_copy.setEnabled(False)
        ctrl_layout.addWidget(self.btn_copy)

        self.btn_export = QPushButton("РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РІ С„Р°Р№Р»...")
        self.btn_export.setObjectName("primary")
        self.btn_export.clicked.connect(self._on_export_clicked)
        self.btn_export.setEnabled(False)
        ctrl_layout.addWidget(self.btn_export)

        ctrl_layout.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(ctrl_layout)

        self.preview = QTextBrowser()
        self.preview.setStyleSheet("background: #2a2a35; border: 1px solid #444; border-radius: 4px; font-family: Consolas, monospace;")
        self.preview.setHtml("<p style='color:#888;'>РќРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…. РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ Р°РЅР°Р»РёР· pcap.</p>")
        layout.addWidget(self.preview)

    def update_data(self, report: dict):
        self._report = report
        self.btn_export.setEnabled(True)
        self.btn_copy.setEnabled(True)
        self._update_preview()

    def _generate_csv(self) -> str:
        import io
        output = io.StringIO()
        writer = csv.writer(output)
        writer.writerow(["Type", "Value", "Severity", "Confidence", "MITRE", "Src IP", "Dst IP", "Description"])
        for f in self._report.get("findings", []):
            writer.writerow([
                f.get("type", ""), f.get("value", ""), f.get("severity", ""),
                f.get("confidence", ""), f.get("mitre_id", ""),
                f.get("src_ip", ""), f.get("dst_ip", ""), f.get("description", "")
            ])
        return output.getvalue()

    def _generate_stix(self) -> str:
        indicators = _extract_stix_iocs(self._report.get("findings", []))
        bundle = {
            "type": "bundle",
            "id": f"bundle--{uuid.uuid4()}",
            "objects": indicators
        }
        return json.dumps(bundle, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)

    def _update_preview(self):
        if not self._report: return

        fmt = self.format_combo.currentData()
        if fmt == "csv":
            self._current_export_data = self._generate_csv()
        else:
            self._current_export_data = self._generate_stix()

        # Р­РєСЂР°РЅРёСЂСѓРµРј HTML-СЃРёРјРІРѕР»С‹ РґР»СЏ Р±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕРіРѕ РѕС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ
        display_text = html.escape(self._current_export_data)

        # РџРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµРј РїРѕР»РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ, Р±РµР· РѕР±СЂРµР·РєРё
        self.preview.setHtml(f"<pre style='color:#d4d4d4;'>{display_text}</pre>")

    def _on_copy_clicked(self):
        if not self._current_export_data:
            return
        clipboard = QApplication.clipboard()
        clipboard.setText(self._current_export_data)
        QMessageBox.information(self, "РЈСЃРїРµС…", "РџРѕР»РЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅС‹ РІ Р±СѓС„РµСЂ РѕР±РјРµРЅР°.\nРњРѕР¶РЅРѕ РІСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏС‚СЊ РІ SIEM РёР»Рё С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІС‹Р№ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ.")

    def _on_export_clicked(self):
        if not self._report: return
        fmt = self.format_combo.currentData()

        if fmt == "csv":
            path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ CSV", "findings.csv", "CSV (*.csv)")
            if not path: return
            try:
                with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write(self._current_export_data)
                QMessageBox.information(self, "РЈСЃРїРµС…", "CSV СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅ.")
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "РћС€РёР±РєР°", str(e))
        else:
            path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ STIX", "bundle.json", "JSON (*.json)")
            if not path: return
            try:
                with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write(self._current_export_data)
                QMessageBox.information(self, "РЈСЃРїРµС…", "STIX Bundle СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅ.")
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "РћС€РёР±РєР°", str(e))
