def _parse_ports(text: str) -> frozenset[int]:
    """'80, 443 8080 # web' в†’ {80, 443, 8080}.

    Р Р°Р·РґРµР»РёС‚РµР»Рё вЂ” РїСЂРѕР±РµР»С‹, Р·Р°РїСЏС‚С‹Рµ, РїРµСЂРµРІРѕРґС‹ СЃС‚СЂРѕРє. РљРѕРјРјРµРЅС‚Р°СЂРёРё (РїРѕСЃР»Рµ #)
    РёРіРЅРѕСЂРёСЂСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ. РќРµРІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹Рµ С‚РѕРєРµРЅС‹ РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ РјРѕР»С‡Р°: GUI РЅРµ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ
    РїР°РґР°С‚СЊ РёР·-Р·Р° РѕРїРµС‡Р°С‚РєРё.
    """
    out: set[int] = set()
    for chunk in text.replace(",", " ").split():
        if chunk.startswith("#"):
            break
        try:
            p = int(chunk)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if 1 <= p <= 65535:
            out.add(p)
    return frozenset(out)


def _parse_ports_with_report(text: str) -> tuple[frozenset[int], list[str]]:
    """РљР°Рє _parse_ports, РЅРѕ РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє РїСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅРЅС‹С… С‚РѕРєРµРЅРѕРІ.

    РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РІ _apply_* РґР»СЏ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєР° В«N РїСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅРѕВ».
    """
    out: set[int] = set()
    skipped: list[str] = []
    for chunk in text.replace(",", " ").split():
        if chunk.startswith("#"):
            break
        try:
            p = int(chunk)
        except ValueError:
            skipped.append(chunk)
            continue
        if 1 <= p <= 65535:
            out.add(p)
        else:
            skipped.append(chunk)
    return frozenset(out), skipped


def _format_ports(ports) -> str:
    """{443, 80} в†’ '80, 443'. РЎРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІРєР° вЂ” СЃС‚Р°Р±РёР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє РІ UI."""
    return ", ".join(str(p) for p in sorted(ports))


def _parse_lines(text: str) -> frozenset[str]:
    """РћРґРёРЅ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚ РЅР° СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ. РџСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё Рё # РєРѕРјРјРµРЅС‚Р°СЂРёРё вЂ” РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРє."""
    return frozenset(
        line.strip() for line in text.splitlines()
        if line.strip() and not line.strip().startswith("#")
    )


def _format_lines(items) -> str:
    """frozenset[str] в†’ РјРЅРѕРіРѕСЃС‚СЂРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚, СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІРєР° РґР»СЏ СЃС‚Р°Р±РёР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё."""
    return "\n".join(sorted(items))


class SettingsView(QWidget):
    # Р”РµС„РѕР»С‚С‹ РґРѕ С‚РѕРіРѕ, РєР°Рє __init__ СѓСЃРїРµРµС‚ РёС… РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ.
    _active_profile_key: str = DEFAULT_PROFILE_KEY
    _search_text: str = ""
    _defaults = None
    thresholds_applied = Signal(object)
    detectors_applied = Signal(object)
    contradictions_applied = Signal(object)
    # РЎРµРєС†РёРё Brute force / РЎРµС‚СЊ / РџСЂРѕРёР·РІРѕРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ / Allowlist / DGA /
    # Threat Intel / РћС‚С‡С‘С‚С‹ С€Р»СЋС‚ РїР»РѕСЃРєРёР№ dict {РїРѕР»Рµ Config: Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ}.
    # run_view РјРµСЂРґР¶РёС‚ РІ РѕР±С‰РёР№ overrides.
    config_applied = Signal(object)

    def __init__(self, parent=None, store: SettingsStore | None = None) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._store = store
        self._secrets = SecretsStore()
        self._build_ui()
        self._apply_help_tooltips()
        self.load_from_store()
        self._active_profile_key = self._read_active_profile()
        self._wire_modified_signals()
        self._check_modified()
        self._wire_modified_signals()
        self._check_modified()
        # РђРІС‚РѕСЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРµ РїСЂРё РєР°Р¶РґРѕРј Apply. РџРѕРґРїРёСЃРєР° РЅР° СЃРІРѕРё Р¶Рµ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»С‹:
        # РЅРµ РЅР°РґРѕ С‚СЂРѕРіР°С‚СЊ _apply_* РјРµС‚РѕРґС‹.
        self.thresholds_applied.connect(self._save_overrides)
        self.config_applied.connect(self._save_overrides)
        self.detectors_applied.connect(self._save_disabled_detectors)
        self.contradictions_applied.connect(self._save_disabled_contradictions)

    def _build_ui(self) -> None:
        outer = QVBoxLayout(self)
        outer.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        outer.setSpacing(0)

        search_row = QHBoxLayout()
        search_row.setContentsMargins(8, 8, 8, 4)
        search_row.setSpacing(6)

        self._search_line = QLineEdit()
        self._search_line.setPlaceholderText(
            "РџРѕРёСЃРє РїРѕ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєР°Рј (Ctrl+F)вЂ¦"
        )
        self._search_line.setClearButtonEnabled(True)
        self._search_line.setToolTip(
            "РС‰РµС‚ РїРѕ РёРјРµРЅРё РїРѕР»СЏ Рё РїРѕ РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёСЋ (tooltip).\n"
            "РџСЂРёРјРµСЂС‹:\n"
            "  beaconing  в†’  РІСЃРµ РїРѕР»СЏ РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂР° beaconing\n"
            "  СЌРЅС‚СЂРѕРї     в†’  DGA: РїРѕСЂРѕРі СЌРЅС‚СЂРѕРїРёРё (СЃРѕРІРїР°РґРµРЅРёРµ РІ РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРё)\n"
            "  nxdomain   в†’  С‚СЂРё РїРѕР»СЏ NXDOMAIN burst\n"
            "\n"
            "Ctrl+F вЂ” С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РІ РїРѕР»Рµ. Esc вЂ” РѕС‡РёСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ."
        )
        self._search_line.textChanged.connect(self._apply_search)
        self._search_line.installEventFilter(self)
        search_row.addWidget(self._search_line, stretch=1)

        self._search_hint = QLabel("")
        self._search_hint.setObjectName("muted")
        search_row.addWidget(self._search_hint)

        outer.addLayout(search_row)

        # Ctrl+F вЂ” С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РІ РїРѕРёСЃРє. РЎС‚Р°РЅРґР°СЂС‚РЅС‹Р№ Р¶РµСЃС‚, РѕР¶РёРґР°РµРјС‹Р№
        # РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»РµРј. Р Р°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚, РєРѕРіРґР° С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё SettingsView.
        from PySide6.QtCore import Qt as _Qt
        from PySide6.QtGui import QKeySequence, QShortcut
        sc = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+F"), self)
        sc.setContext(_Qt.ShortcutContext.WidgetWithChildrenShortcut)
        sc.activated.connect(self._focus_search)
        # Р”РµСЂР¶РёРј СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ С€РѕСЂС‚РєР°С‚ РЅРµ СЃРѕР±СЂР°Р»СЃСЏ GC.
        self._search_shortcut = sc

        layout = QHBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(0)

        self._sidebar = self._build_sidebar()
        layout.addWidget(self._sidebar)

        self._stack = QStackedWidget()
        self._build_pages()
        layout.addWidget(self._stack, stretch=1)
        outer.addLayout(layout, stretch=1)

        # РЎС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ-РїРѕР»РѕСЃР°: СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє РїСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅРЅС‹С… С‚РѕРєРµРЅРѕРІ, РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёРµ РїРѕРґСЃРєР°Р·РєРё.
        self._status_label = QLabel("")
        self._status_label.setObjectName("muted")
        self._status_label.setContentsMargins(12, 4, 12, 4)
        outer.addWidget(self._status_label)

        self._sidebar.currentRowChanged.connect(self._stack.setCurrentIndex)
        self._sidebar.setCurrentRow(0)

    def _build_sidebar(self) -> QListWidget:
        w = QListWidget()
        w.setObjectName("settingsSidebar")
        w.setFixedWidth(200)
        for key, label in _SECTIONS:
            item = QListWidgetItem(label)
            item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, key)
            w.addItem(item)
        return w

    def _build_pages(self) -> None:
        for key, label in _SECTIONS:
            if key == "thresholds":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_thresholds_page())
            elif key == "detectors":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_detectors_page())
            elif key == "contradictions":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_contradictions_page())
            elif key == "brute":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_brute_force_page())
            elif key == "network":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_network_page())
            elif key == "perf":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_performance_page())
            elif key == "allowlist":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_allowlist_page())
            elif key == "dga":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_dga_page())
            elif key == "yara":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_yara_page())
            elif key == "reports":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_reports_page())
            elif key == "yara":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_yara_page())
            elif key == "yara":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_yara_page())
            elif key == "threatintel":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_threatintel_page())
            elif key == "general":
                self._stack.addWidget(self._build_general_page())
            else:
                self._stack.addWidget(self._placeholder_page(key, label))

    def _placeholder_page(self, key: str, title: str) -> QWidget:
        """Р—Р°РіР»СѓС€РєР° СЃРµРєС†РёРё СЃ РѕСЃРјС‹СЃР»РµРЅРЅС‹Рј РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµРј.

        РљР°Р¶РґР°СЏ Р·Р°РіР»СѓС€РєР° РѕР±СЉСЏСЃРЅСЏРµС‚, С‡С‚Рѕ Р·РґРµСЃСЊ Р±СѓРґРµС‚ Рё Р·Р°С‡РµРј. Р­С‚Рѕ Р»СѓС‡С€Рµ
        Р±РµР·Р»РёРєРѕРіРѕ В«РІ СЂР°Р·СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєРµВ»: РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ РІРёРґРёС‚ СЃРјС‹СЃР».
        """
        w = QWidget()
        v = QVBoxLayout(w)
        v.setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24)
        v.setSpacing(12)

        h = QLabel(title)
        h.setObjectName("h1")
        v.addWidget(h)

        body = _PLACEHOLDER_TEXT.get(key, "РЎРµРєС†РёСЏ РІ СЂР°Р·СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєРµ.")
        hint = QLabel(body)
        hint.setObjectName("muted")
        hint.setWordWrap(True)
        hint.setTextFormat(Qt.TextFormat.RichText)
        v.addWidget(hint)
        v.addStretch(1)
        return w

    def _apply_help_tooltips(self) -> None:
        """РџРѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ tooltip'С‹ РЅР° РїРѕР»СЏ Рё Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєРё СЃРµРєС†РёР№.

        Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєРё: tooltip РІРµС€Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РЅР° QLabel РёР· _make_header РїРѕ
        РєР°СЂС‚Рµ SECTION_BY_TITLE. РџРѕР»СЏ: РѕР±С…РѕРґ РІСЃРµС… *_inputs-РіСЂСѓРїРї,
        tooltip РёР· get_field_help.
        """
        for group_name in (
            "_threshold_inputs", "_brute_inputs", "_network_inputs",
            "_perf_inputs", "_allowlist_inputs", "_dga_inputs",
            "_reports_inputs", "_ti_inputs",
        ):
            group = getattr(self, group_name, None)
            if not isinstance(group, dict):
                continue
            for key, widget in group.items():
                if widget is None:
                    continue
                try:
                    widget.setToolTip(get_field_help(key))
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # =========================================================================
    # Р‘Р•Р™Р”Р– В«РР—РњР•РќР•РќРћВ» Р РЎРўРђРўРЈРЎ-РџРћР›РћРЎРђ
    # =========================================================================

    _MARK_STYLE = "color: #d0a060;"

    _INPUT_GROUPS = (
        "_threshold_inputs", "_brute_inputs", "_network_inputs",
        "_perf_inputs", "_allowlist_inputs", "_dga_inputs",
        "_reports_inputs", "_ti_inputs",
    )

    def _notify_status(self, msg: str, level: str = "info",
                       timeout_ms: int = 4000) -> None:
        label = getattr(self, "_status_label", None)
        if label is None:
            return
        color = {"warn": "#d0a060", "error": "#e06060"}.get(level, "")
        label.setStyleSheet(f"color: {color};" if color else "")
        label.setText(msg)

        from PySide6.QtCore import QTimer

        def _clear():
            try:
                label.setText("")
            except RuntimeError:
                pass

        QTimer.singleShot(timeout_ms, _clear)

    def _wire_modified_signals(self) -> None:
        for group_name in self._INPUT_GROUPS:
            group = getattr(self, group_name, None)
            if not isinstance(group, dict):
                continue
            for widget in group.values():
                if isinstance(widget, (QSpinBox, QDoubleSpinBox)):
                    widget.valueChanged.connect(self._check_modified)
                elif isinstance(widget, QCheckBox):
                    widget.stateChanged.connect(self._check_modified)
                elif isinstance(widget, QLineEdit):
                    widget.textChanged.connect(self._check_modified)
                elif isinstance(widget, (QPlainTextEdit, EditableListWidget)):
                    widget.textChanged.connect(self._check_modified)

    def _check_modified(self, *args) -> None:
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
            self._defaults = Config()
        except ImportError:
            self._defaults = None
        for group_name in self._INPUT_GROUPS:
            group = getattr(self, group_name, None)
            if not isinstance(group, dict):
                continue
            for key, widget in group.items():
                self._refresh_widget_style(widget, key)

    def _refresh_widget_style(self, widget, key: str) -> None:
        """РЎРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ modified (С†РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°) Рё search (С„РѕРЅ)."""
        parts: list[str] = []
        if self._defaults is not None:
            default = getattr(self._defaults, key, None)
            if default is not None:
                try:
                    if self._widget_differs(widget, default):
                        parts.append(self._MARK_STYLE)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        if self._search_text and self._is_search_match(widget, key):
            parts.append("background-color: #4a4a10;")
        widget.setStyleSheet(" ".join(parts))

    def _is_search_match(self, widget, key: str) -> bool:
        if not self._search_text:
            return False
        hay = (key + " " + (widget.toolTip() or "")).lower()
        return self._search_text in hay

    def _focus_search(self) -> None:
        self._search_line.setFocus()
        self._search_line.selectAll()

    def eventFilter(self, obj, event):
        """Esc РІ РїРѕР»Рµ РїРѕРёСЃРєР°: РµСЃР»Рё С‚РµРєСЃС‚ РµСЃС‚СЊ вЂ” РѕС‡РёСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ, РёРЅР°С‡Рµ blur."""
        from PySide6.QtCore import QEvent
        from PySide6.QtCore import Qt as _Qt
        if obj is getattr(self, "_search_line", None):
            if event.type() == QEvent.Type.KeyPress:
                if event.key() == _Qt.Key.Key_Escape:
                    if self._search_line.text():
                        self._search_line.clear()
                    else:
                        self._search_line.clearFocus()
                    return True
        return super().eventFilter(obj, event)

    def _apply_search(self, text: str) -> None:
        self._search_text = (text or "").strip().lower()
        self._check_modified()
        hint = getattr(self, "_search_hint", None)
        if not self._search_text:
            self._notify_status("")
            if hint is not None:
                hint.setText("")
            return
        count = sum(
            1
            for g in self._INPUT_GROUPS
            for k, w in getattr(self, g, {}).items()
            if self._is_search_match(w, k)
        )
        msg = f"РќР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ РїРѕР»РµР№: {count}"
        if hint is not None:
            hint.setText(msg)
        self._notify_status(msg)

    @staticmethod
    def _widget_differs(widget, default) -> bool:
        from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
            QCheckBox, QDoubleSpinBox, QLineEdit,
            QPlainTextEdit, QSpinBox,
        )
        if isinstance(widget, QSpinBox):
            return int(widget.value()) != int(default)
        if isinstance(widget, QDoubleSpinBox):
            return abs(float(widget.value()) - float(default)) > 1e-9
        if isinstance(widget, QCheckBox):
            return bool(widget.isChecked()) != bool(default)
        if isinstance(widget, QLineEdit):
            return widget.text().strip() != str(default)
        if isinstance(widget, EditableListWidget):
            current_list = widget.items()
            if isinstance(default, tuple):
                return tuple(current_list) != default
            if isinstance(default, frozenset):
                if default and isinstance(next(iter(default)), int):
                    try:
                        current_set = frozenset(int(x) for x in current_list)
                    except ValueError:
                        return True
                    return current_set != default
                return frozenset(current_list) != default
            return False
        if isinstance(widget, QPlainTextEdit):
            text = widget.toPlainText()
            if isinstance(default, tuple):
                current = tuple(
                    line.strip() for line in text.splitlines()
                    if line.strip() and not line.strip().startswith("#")
                )
                return current != default
            if isinstance(default, frozenset):
                if default and isinstance(next(iter(default)), int):
                    current = _parse_ports(text)
                else:
                    current = _parse_lines(text)
                return current != default
        return False

    def goto(self, key: str) -> None:
        for i in range(self._sidebar.count()):
            item = self._sidebar.item(i)
            if item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) == key:
                self._sidebar.setCurrentRow(i)
                return

    # =========================================================================
    # РћР‘Р©РР• РҐР•Р›РџР•Р Р« РџРћРЎРўР РћР•РќРРЇ РЎР•РљР¦РР™
    # =========================================================================

    def _make_scroll_form(self):
        """QScrollArea + QFormLayout РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё. Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ (scroll, form, host).

        Р’СЃРµ СЃРµРєС†РёРё РѕРґРЅРѕС‚РёРїРЅС‹: Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє, РїРѕРґРїРёСЃСЊ, РїСЂРѕРєСЂСѓС‡РёРІР°РµРјР°СЏ С„РѕСЂРјР°,
        РєРЅРѕРїРєРё СЃРЅРёР·Сѓ. РҐРµР»РїРµСЂ СѓР±РёСЂР°РµС‚ РґСѓР±Р»РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ.
        """
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setFrameShape(QScrollArea.Shape.NoFrame)

        host = QWidget()
        form = QFormLayout(host)
        form.setContentsMargins(0, 8, 0, 8)
        form.setSpacing(14)
        # Label СЃР»РµРІР°, РІРµСЂС…РЅРµРµ РІС‹СЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РЅРёРµ вЂ” С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ РїРѕР»СЏ Р±С‹Р»Рѕ
        # РЅР° РѕРґРЅРѕРј СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµ СЃРѕ СЃРїРёРЅРЅРµСЂРѕРј, Р° РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ СѓС…РѕРґРёР»Рѕ РїРѕРґ РЅРёРј.
        form.setLabelAlignment(
            Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop
        )
        form.setFormAlignment(
            Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop
        )
        # FieldsStayAtSizeHint вЂ” СЃРїРёРЅРЅРµСЂС‹ С„РёРєСЃ. С€РёСЂРёРЅС‹. Label-РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєРµ
        # СЌС‚Рѕ РЅРµ РјРµС€Р°РµС‚ СЂР°СЃС‚СЏРіРёРІР°С‚СЊСЃСЏ: Сѓ label-РІРёРґР¶РµС‚Р° Expanding, Р°
        # С„РѕСЂРјР° РїСЂРё РЅРµС…РІР°С‚РєРµ РјРµСЃС‚Р° РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ СЃРІРѕР±РѕРґРЅРѕРµ РјРµСЃС‚Рѕ РµРјСѓ.
        form.setFieldGrowthPolicy(
            QFormLayout.FieldGrowthPolicy.FieldsStayAtSizeHint
        )
        form.setRowWrapPolicy(QFormLayout.RowWrapPolicy.DontWrapRows)
        scroll.setWidget(host)
        return scroll, form

    def _make_header(self, title: str, subtitle: str) -> tuple[QLabel, QLabel]:
        t = QLabel(title)
        t.setObjectName("h1")
        s = QLabel(subtitle)
        s.setObjectName("muted")
        s.setWordWrap(True)
        section_key = SECTION_BY_TITLE.get(title)
        if section_key:
            help_text = get_section_help(section_key)
            if help_text:
                t.setToolTip(help_text)
                s.setToolTip(help_text)
        return t, s

    def _make_buttons(self, reset_slot, apply_slot) -> QHBoxLayout:
        h = QHBoxLayout()
        h.addStretch(1)
        rst = QPushButton("РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ Рє default")
        rst.clicked.connect(reset_slot)
        apt = QPushButton("РџСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ")
        apt.setObjectName("primary")
        apt.clicked.connect(apply_slot)
        h.addWidget(rst)
        h.addWidget(apt)
        return h

    # =========================================================================
    # РџР РћР¤РР›Р
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_profile_row(self):
        from PySide6.QtWidgets import QComboBox, QLabel, QHBoxLayout, QPushButton

        row = QHBoxLayout()
        row.setSpacing(8)

        row.addWidget(QLabel("РџСЂРѕС„РёР»СЊ:"))
        self._profile_combo = QComboBox()
        self._profile_combo.setFixedWidth(240)
        for p in ALL_PROFILES:
            self._profile_combo.addItem(p.title, p.key)
        # РЈСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёР№
        idx = self._profile_combo.findData(self._active_profile_key)
        if idx >= 0:
            self._profile_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        self._profile_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(
            self._on_profile_combo_changed
        )
        row.addWidget(self._profile_combo)

        apply_btn = QPushButton("РџСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ РїСЂРѕС„РёР»СЊ")
        apply_btn.clicked.connect(self._apply_active_profile)
        row.addWidget(apply_btn)

        row.addStretch(1)

        self._profile_hint = QLabel("")
        self._profile_hint.setObjectName("muted")
        self._profile_hint.setWordWrap(True)
        row.addWidget(self._profile_hint, stretch=2)

        self._update_profile_hint()
        return row

    def _on_profile_combo_changed(self, index: int) -> None:
        key = self._profile_combo.itemData(index) or DEFAULT_PROFILE_KEY
        self._active_profile_key = key
        self._update_profile_hint()

    def _update_profile_hint(self) -> None:
        p = get_profile(self._active_profile_key)
        if p is None:
            self._profile_hint.setText("")
            return
        n_ov = len(p.overrides)
        n_dd = len(p.disabled_detectors)
        n_dc = len(p.disabled_contradictions)
        parts = [p.description]
        if n_ov or n_dd or n_dc:
            parts.append(
                f"(РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂРѕРІ: {n_ov}, "
                f"РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡С‘РЅРЅС‹С… РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ: {n_dd}, "
                f"РїСЂР°РІРёР»: {n_dc})"
            )
        self._profile_hint.setText(" ".join(parts))

    def _apply_active_profile(self) -> None:
        """РџСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРѕС„РёР»СЊ: Р·Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚СЊ РІРёРґР¶РµС‚С‹ Рё СЌРјРёС‚РёС‚СЊ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»С‹."""
        p = get_profile(self._active_profile_key)
        if p is None:
            return

        # 1) Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚СЊ РІРёРґР¶РµС‚С‹ РџРѕСЂРѕРіРѕРІ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏРјРё РёР· РїСЂРѕС„РёР»СЏ,
        #    РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ вЂ” РґРµС„РѕР»С‚Р°РјРё РёР· Config.
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
            cfg = Config()
        except ImportError:
            cfg = None

        for key, widget in self._threshold_inputs.items():
            if key in p.overrides:
                value = p.overrides[key]
            elif cfg is not None:
                value = getattr(cfg, key, None)
            else:
                continue
            self._set_widget_value(widget, value, key)

        # 2) РћР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ С‡РµРєР±РѕРєСЃС‹ РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ Рё РїСЂРѕС‚РёРІРѕСЂРµС‡РёР№.
        for name, cb in self._detector_boxes.items():
            cb.setChecked(name not in p.disabled_detectors)
        for name, cb in self._contradiction_boxes.items():
            cb.setChecked(name not in p.disabled_contradictions)

        # 3) РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРѕС„РёР»СЊ Рё СЌРјРёС‚РёС‚СЊ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»С‹.
        self._save_active_profile(p.key)

        thresholds_values = {}
        for key, widget in self._threshold_inputs.items():
            if hasattr(widget, "value"):
                thresholds_values[key] = widget.value()
        if thresholds_values:
            self.thresholds_applied.emit(thresholds_values)

        self.detectors_applied.emit(set(p.disabled_detectors))
        self.contradictions_applied.emit(set(p.disabled_contradictions))

    def _read_active_profile(self) -> str:
        if self._store is None:
            return DEFAULT_PROFILE_KEY
        data = self._store.load()
        key = data.get("active_profile", DEFAULT_PROFILE_KEY)
        return key if get_profile(key) is not None else DEFAULT_PROFILE_KEY

    def _save_active_profile(self, key: str) -> None:
        if self._store is None:
            return
        current = self._store.load()
        current["active_profile"] = key
        self._store.save(current)

    # РЎР•РљР¦РРЇ: РџРѕСЂРѕРіРё
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_general_page(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        outer = QVBoxLayout(w)
        outer.setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24)
        outer.setSpacing(12)

        t, s = self._make_header(
            "РћР±С‰РёРµ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё",
            "Р‘Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Рµ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂС‹ РёРЅС‚РµСЂС„РµР№СЃР°.",
        )
        outer.addWidget(t)
        outer.addWidget(s)

        form = QFormLayout()
        form.setSpacing(14)

        self._lang_combo = QComboBox()
        self._lang_combo.addItem("Р СѓСЃСЃРєРёР№", "ru")
        self._lang_combo.addItem("English", "en")

        # Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёР№ СЏР·С‹Рє
        if self._store:
            current_lang = self._store.load().get("language", "ru")
            idx = self._lang_combo.findData(current_lang)
            if idx >= 0:
                self._lang_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)

        self._lang_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._on_lang_changed)
        form.addRow("РЇР·С‹Рє РёРЅС‚РµСЂС„РµР№СЃР°:", self._lang_combo)
        outer.addLayout(form)
        outer.addStretch(1)
        return w

    def _on_lang_changed(self, index: int) -> None:
        lang = self._lang_combo.itemData(index)
        if self._store and lang:
            self._store.save_partial(language=lang)
            QMessageBox.information(
                self, "РЇР·С‹Рє",
                "РЇР·С‹Рє РёР·РјРµРЅС‘РЅ. Р”Р»СЏ РїРѕР»РЅРѕРіРѕ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїСѓСЃС‚РёС‚Рµ РїСЂРѕРіСЂР°РјРјСѓ.",
            )

    def _label_with_hint(self, label_text: str, key: str):
        """QWidget: [label СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ] [hint РїРѕРґ РЅРёРј СЃРµСЂС‹Рј].

        РҐРѕСЃС‚ СЂР°СЃС‚СЏРіРёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РїРѕ РіРѕСЂРёР·РѕРЅС‚Р°Р»Рё (Expanding), С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ QFormLayout
        РѕС‚РґР°Р» РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёСЋ РІСЃСЋ РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅСѓСЋ С€РёСЂРёРЅСѓ. РЎРїРёРЅРЅРµСЂ СЃРїСЂР°РІР° РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ
        С„РёРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕР№ С€РёСЂРёРЅС‹ (160px) вЂ” РѕРЅ РЅРµ СѓС‡Р°СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ РІ stretch.
        """
        from PySide6.QtWidgets import QSizePolicy
        from paradox_gui.field_help import get_field_help

        host = QWidget()
        host.setSizePolicy(
            QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Preferred,
        )
        # QFormLayout РїСЂРё FieldsStayAtSizeHint РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ label-РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєРµ
        # СЂРѕРІРЅРѕ sizeHint. Р‘РµР· СЏРІРЅРѕРіРѕ РјРёРЅРёРјСѓРјР° Expanding РёРіРЅРѕСЂРёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ.
        host.setMinimumWidth(620)
        v = QVBoxLayout(host)
        v.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 12, 6)
        v.setSpacing(2)

        lbl = QLabel(label_text)
        v.addWidget(lbl)

        text = get_field_help(key)
        hint = QLabel(text)
        hint.setWordWrap(True)
        hint.setSizePolicy(
            QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Preferred,
        )
        hint.setStyleSheet("color: #8a8a9a; font-size: 11px;")
        hint.setToolTip(text)
        v.addWidget(hint)

        if not hasattr(self, "_field_hints"):
            self._field_hints: dict[str, QLabel] = {}
        self._field_hints[key] = hint
        return host



    def _add_int_row(self, form, key: str, label: str,
                     lo: int = 0, hi: int = 1_000_000_000) -> None:
        sp = QSpinBox()
        sp.setRange(lo, hi)
        sp.setFixedWidth(160)
        form.addRow(self._label_with_hint(label, key), sp)
        self._threshold_inputs[key] = sp

    def _add_float_row(self, form, key: str, label: str,
                       step: float = 0.1, lo: float = 0.0,
                       hi: float = 1000.0) -> None:
        sp = QDoubleSpinBox()
        sp.setRange(lo, hi)
        sp.setSingleStep(step)
        sp.setDecimals(3)
        sp.setFixedWidth(160)
        form.addRow(self._label_with_hint(label, key), sp)
        self._threshold_inputs[key] = sp

    def _load_thresholds(self) -> None:
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
        except ImportError:
            return
        cfg = Config()
        for key, widget in self._threshold_inputs.items():
            value = getattr(cfg, key, None)
            if value is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(widget, QSpinBox):
                widget.setValue(int(value))
            elif isinstance(widget, QDoubleSpinBox):
                widget.setValue(float(value))

    def _reset_thresholds(self) -> None:
        self._load_thresholds()

    def _apply_thresholds(self) -> None:
        values = {}
        for key, widget in self._threshold_inputs.items():
            if isinstance(widget, QSpinBox):
                values[key] = widget.value()
            elif isinstance(widget, QDoubleSpinBox):
                values[key] = widget.value()
        self.thresholds_applied.emit(values)

    # =========================================================================
    # РЎР•РљР¦РРЇ: Brute force
    # =========================================================================

    def _build_brute_force_page(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        outer = QVBoxLayout(w)
        outer.setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24)
        outer.setSpacing(12)

        t, s = self._make_header(
            "Brute force",
            "РљР°Рє РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ РѕС‚Р»РёС‡Р°РµС‚ РїРѕРґР±РѕСЂ РїР°СЂРѕР»СЏ РѕС‚ РѕР±С‹С‡РЅРѕРіРѕ С‚СЂР°С„РёРєР°. "
            "РџРѕСЂРѕРіРё В«СЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ flowsВ» вЂ” РІ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»Рµ РџРѕСЂРѕРіРё.",
        )
        outer.addWidget(t)
        outer.addWidget(s)

        scroll, form = self._make_scroll_form()
        self._brute_inputs: dict[str, object] = {}

        self._add_int_row_bf(form, "brute_force_min_unique_src_ports",
                             "HTTP: РјРёРЅ. СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… src-РїРѕСЂС‚РѕРІ", lo=1, hi=10000)
        self._add_float_row_bf(form, "brute_force_uri_ratio_threshold",
                               "HTTP: РґРѕР»СЏ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ Рє login-endpoint",
                               lo=0.01, hi=1.0, step=0.05, decimals=2)
        self._add_float_row_bf(form, "brute_force_min_rate_flows_per_sec",
                               "HTTP: РјРёРЅ. СЃРєРѕСЂРѕСЃС‚СЊ (flows/СЃРµРє)",
                               lo=0.001, hi=1000.0, step=0.01, decimals=3)
        self._add_int_row_bf(form, "ftp_brute_force_min_unique_src_ports",
                             "FTP: РјРёРЅ. СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… src-РїРѕСЂС‚РѕРІ", lo=1, hi=10000)

        self._bf_skip_tls = EditableListWidget(port_mode=True)
        form.addRow("HTTP: РїРѕСЂС‚С‹, РіРґРµ brute force РЅРµ РёС‰РµРј (TLS)", self._bf_skip_tls)
        self._brute_inputs["brute_force_skip_tls_ports"] = self._bf_skip_tls

        self._bf_login_hints = EditableListWidget(port_mode=False)
        form.addRow("HTTP: РїРѕРґСЃРєР°Р·РєРё login-endpoint", self._bf_login_hints)
        self._brute_inputs["login_endpoint_hints"] = self._bf_login_hints

        outer.addWidget(scroll, stretch=1)
        outer.addLayout(self._make_buttons(self._load_brute_force, self._apply_brute_force))

        self._load_brute_force()
        return w

    def _add_int_row_bf(self, form, key, label, lo, hi):
        sp = QSpinBox()
        sp.setRange(lo, hi)
        sp.setFixedWidth(160)
        form.addRow(self._label_with_hint(label, key), sp)
        self._brute_inputs[key] = sp

    def _add_float_row_bf(self, form, key, label, lo, hi, step, decimals):
        sp = QDoubleSpinBox()
        sp.setRange(lo, hi)
        sp.setSingleStep(step)
        sp.setDecimals(decimals)
        sp.setFixedWidth(160)
        form.addRow(self._label_with_hint(label, key), sp)
        self._brute_inputs[key] = sp

    def _load_brute_force(self) -> None:
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
        except ImportError:
            return
        cfg = Config()
        for key, widget in self._brute_inputs.items():
            value = getattr(cfg, key, None)
            if value is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(widget, QSpinBox):
                widget.setValue(int(value))
            elif isinstance(widget, QDoubleSpinBox):
                widget.setValue(float(value))
            elif isinstance(widget, (QPlainTextEdit, EditableListWidget)):
                if isinstance(value, frozenset):
                    if value and isinstance(next(iter(value)), int):
                        widget.setPlainText(_format_ports(value))
                    else:
                        widget.setPlainText(_format_lines(value))

    def _apply_brute_force(self) -> None:
        values: dict = {}
        skipped_total = 0
        previews: list[str] = []
        for key, widget in self._brute_inputs.items():
            if isinstance(widget, QSpinBox):
                values[key] = widget.value()
            elif isinstance(widget, QDoubleSpinBox):
                values[key] = widget.value()
            elif isinstance(widget, QPlainTextEdit):
                text = widget.toPlainText()
                if "ports" in key or "skip_tls" in key:
                    parsed, skipped = _parse_ports_with_report(text)
                    values[key] = parsed
                    skipped_total += len(skipped)
                    if skipped:
                        previews.append(f"{key}: {', '.join(skipped[:3])}")
                else:
                    values[key] = _parse_lines(text)
        if skipped_total:
            self._notify_status(
                f"РџСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅРѕ РЅРµРІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹С… С‚РѕРєРµРЅРѕРІ: {skipped_total} "
                f"({'; '.join(previews[:2])})",
                level="warn",
            )
        self.config_applied.emit(values)

    def _build_network_page(self) -> QWidget:

    def _add_smart_net_row(self, form, key, label, is_net=False):
        container = QWidget()
        vbox = QVBoxLayout(container)
        vbox.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        vbox.setSpacing(4)

        te = QPlainTextEdit()
        te.setFixedHeight(60)
        te.setPlaceholderText("РќР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ: 10.0.0.0/8" if is_net else "РќР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ: 80, 443, 8080")

        status_lbl = QLabel("")
        status_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #a0a0b0; font-size: 11px;")
        status_lbl.setWordWrap(True)

        vbox.addWidget(te)
        vbox.addWidget(status_lbl)
        form.addRow(label, container)

        self._network_inputs[key] = te
        self._network_status[key] = status_lbl

        te.setProperty("is_net", is_net)
        te.textChanged.connect(lambda w=te, k=key: self._validate_and_preview_net(w, k))

    def _validate_and_preview_net(self, widget: QPlainTextEdit, key: str) -> None:
        text = widget.toPlainText().strip()
        is_net = widget.property("is_net")
        status_lbl = self._network_status[key]

        if not text:
            widget.setStyleSheet("")
            status_lbl.setText("")
            return

        tokens = [t for t in re.split(r'[\s,]+', text) if t]

        if is_net:
            valid, invalid = [], []
            for t in tokens:
                if re.match(r'^[0-9\./]+$', t):
                    valid.append(t)
                else:
                    invalid.append(t)

            if invalid:
                widget.setStyleSheet("border: 1px solid #ff5555;")
                status_lbl.setText(f"<span style='color:#ff5555;'>РћС€РёР±РєР° РІ: {', '.join(set(invalid))}</span>")
            else:
                widget.setStyleSheet("")
                status_lbl.setText(f"Р‘СѓРґРµС‚ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРѕ: {', '.join(valid)}")
        else:
            valid, invalid = [], []
            for t in tokens:
                if t.isdigit() and 1 <= int(t) <= 65535:
                    valid.append(str(int(t)))
                else:
                    invalid.append(t)

            if invalid:
                widget.setStyleSheet("border: 1px solid #ff5555;")
                status_lbl.setText(f"<span style='color:#ff5555;'>РќРµРІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕСЂС‚С‹: {', '.join(set(invalid))}</span>")
            else:
                sorted_valid = sorted(list(set(int(p) for p in valid)))
                widget.setStyleSheet("")
                status_lbl.setText(f"Р‘СѓРґРµС‚ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРѕ ({len(sorted_valid)}): {', '.join(map(str, sorted_valid))}")

    def _load_network(self) -> None:
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
            cfg = Config()
        except ImportError:
            return
        for key, widget in self._network_inputs.items():
            value = getattr(cfg, key, None)
            if value is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(value, frozenset):
                if value and isinstance(next(iter(value)), int):
                    widget.setPlainText(_format_ports(value))
                else:
                    widget.setPlainText(_format_lines(value))
            elif isinstance(value, tuple):
                widget.setPlainText("\n".join(value))

    def _apply_network(self) -> None:
        values: dict = {}
        for key, widget in self._network_inputs.items():
            text = widget.toPlainText().strip()
            is_net = widget.property("is_net")
            tokens = [t for t in re.split(r'[\s,]+', text) if t]

            if is_net:
                valid = tuple(t for t in tokens if re.match(r'^[0-9\./]+$', t))
                values[key] = valid
            else:
                valid_ports = set()
                for t in tokens:
                    if t.isdigit() and 1 <= int(t) <= 65535:
                        valid_ports.add(int(t))
                values[key] = frozenset(valid_ports)

        self.config_applied.emit(values)

    def _load_performance(self) -> None:
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
            cfg = Config()
            for key, widget in self._perf_inputs.items():
                val = getattr(cfg, key, None)
                if val is not None and isinstance(widget, QCheckBox):
                    widget.setChecked(bool(val))
        except ImportError:
            pass

    def _apply_performance(self) -> None:
        values = {}
        for key, widget in self._perf_inputs.items():
            if isinstance(widget, QCheckBox):
                values[key] = widget.isChecked()
        self.config_applied.emit(values)


    def _add_bool_row(self, form, key, label):
        cb = QCheckBox()
        form.addRow(self._label_with_hint(label, key), cb)
        self._perf_inputs[key] = cb

    def _add_int_row_pf(self, form, key, label, lo, hi):
        sp = QSpinBox()
        sp.setRange(lo, hi)
        sp.setFixedWidth(180)
        form.addRow(self._label_with_hint(label, key), sp)
        self._perf_inputs[key] = sp

    def _load_performance(self) -> None:
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
        except ImportError:
            return
        cfg = Config()
        for key, widget in self._perf_inputs.items():
            value = getattr(cfg, key, None)
            if value is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(widget, QCheckBox):
                widget.setChecked(bool(value))
            elif isinstance(widget, QSpinBox):
                widget.setValue(int(value))

    def _apply_performance(self) -> None:
        values: dict = {}
        for key, widget in self._perf_inputs.items():
            if isinstance(widget, QCheckBox):
                values[key] = widget.isChecked()
            elif isinstance(widget, QSpinBox):
                values[key] = widget.value()
        self.config_applied.emit(values)

    def _add_list_row_al(self, form, key, label, height=90):
        from paradox_gui.field_help import get_field_help
        w = EditableListWidget(
            port_mode=(key in PORT_FIELDS),

        )
        form.addRow(label, w)
        self._allowlist_inputs[key] = w

    def _load_allowlist(self) -> None:
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
        except ImportError:
            return
        cfg = Config()
        for key, widget in self._allowlist_inputs.items():
            value = getattr(cfg, key, None)
            if value is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(value, (frozenset, tuple, set, list)):
                widget.setPlainText(_format_lines(value))

    def _apply_allowlist(self) -> None:
        values: dict = {}
        for key, widget in self._allowlist_inputs.items():
            values[key] = _parse_lines(widget.toPlainText())
        self.config_applied.emit(values)

    # РЎР•РљР¦РРЇ: YARA
    # =========================================================================




    def _validate_yara_rules(self) -> None:
        try:
            import yara
        except ImportError:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "YARA", "РњРѕРґСѓР»СЊ yara-python РЅРµ СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅ.\nР—Р°РїСѓСЃС‚РёС‚Рµ: pip install yara-python")
            return

        path = self._yara_path.text().strip()
        if not path:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "YARA", "РЈРєР°Р¶РёС‚Рµ РїСѓС‚СЊ Рє С„Р°Р№Р»Сѓ РїСЂР°РІРёР» (.yar)")
            return

        try:
            rules = yara.compile(filepath=path)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "YARA", f"РЈСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ! Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶РµРЅРѕ РїСЂР°РІРёР»: {sum(1 for _ in rules)}")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "РћС€РёР±РєР° РІ РїСЂР°РІРёР»Р°С…", f"{type(e).__name__}:\n{e}")



    # =========================================================================
    # РЎР•РљР¦РРЇ: РћС‚С‡С‘С‚С‹
    # =========================================================================

            "РїСЂРё РІРєР»СЋС‡С‘РЅРЅРѕР№ Р·Р°РїРёСЃРё). РџСѓС‚СЊ СЃРѕР·РґР°С‘С‚СЃСЏ РїСЂРё РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё.",

    def _load_reports(self) -> None:
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
        except ImportError:
            return
        cfg = Config()
        value = getattr(cfg, "output_dir", None)
        if value is not None:
            self._output_dir.setText(str(value))

    def _apply_reports(self) -> None:
        text = self._output_dir.text().strip() or "reports"
        path = Path(text)
        # РЎРѕР·РґР°С‘Рј СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ: Config.__post_init__ РґРµР»Р°РµС‚ mkdir С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ
        # РґРµС„РѕР»С‚РЅРѕРіРѕ РїСѓС‚Рё. РџСЂРё override С‡РµСЂРµР· object.__setattr__ РїРѕСЃС‚-
        # РёРЅРёС†РёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ РЅРµ Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєР°РµС‚СЃСЏ, РїРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ РїР°РїРєР° РѕСЃС‚Р°РЅРµС‚СЃСЏ РЅРµ
        # СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРЅРѕР№, Рё СЏРґСЂРѕ СѓРїР°РґС‘С‚ РїСЂРё РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєРµ РїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ.
        try:
            path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        except OSError:
            # РќРµ РєСЂРёС‚РёС‡РЅРѕ: РµСЃР»Рё РїСѓС‚СЊ РЅРµРІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹Р№, СЏРґСЂРѕ СЃР°РјРѕ РѕС‚РІР°Р»РёС‚СЃСЏ
            # РїРѕР·Р¶Рµ СЃ РїРѕРЅСЏС‚РЅРѕР№ РѕС€РёР±РєРѕР№. РњРѕР»С‡Р° РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј.
            pass
        self.config_applied.emit({"output_dir": path})

    def _build_yara_page(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        outer = QVBoxLayout(w)
        outer.setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24)
        outer.setSpacing(12)

        t, s = self._make_header(
            "YARA",
            "РџРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊСЃРєРёРµ YARA-РїСЂР°РІРёР»Р° РґР»СЏ РїРѕРёСЃРєР° СЃРёРіРЅР°С‚СѓСЂ РІ payload. "
            "Р•СЃР»Рё С„Р°Р№Р» РЅРµ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅ, РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ СЃС‚Р°РЅРґР°СЂС‚РЅС‹Рµ РїСЂР°РІРёР»Р° РёР· СЏРґСЂР°.",
        )
        outer.addWidget(t)
        outer.addWidget(s)

        scroll, form = self._make_scroll_form()
        self._yara_inputs = {}

        self._yara_path = QLineEdit()
        self._yara_path.setPlaceholderText("РџРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ (rules/default.yar)")
        self._yara_path.setFixedWidth(420)

        btn_browse = QPushButton("РћР±Р·РѕСЂвЂ¦")
        btn_browse.clicked.connect(self._browse_yara_file)

        btn_validate = QPushButton("РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРёС‚СЊ РїСЂР°РІРёР»Р°")
        btn_validate.clicked.connect(self._validate_yara_rules)

        row = QHBoxLayout()
        row.addWidget(self._yara_path)
        row.addWidget(btn_browse)
        row.addWidget(btn_validate)
        row.addStretch(1)

        form.addRow("РџСѓС‚СЊ Рє С„Р°Р№Р»Сѓ РїСЂР°РІРёР» (.yar):", row)
        self._yara_inputs["yara_rules_path"] = self._yara_path

        outer.addWidget(scroll, stretch=1)
        outer.addLayout(self._make_buttons(self._load_yara, self._apply_yara))

        self._load_yara()
        return w

    def _browse_yara_file(self) -> None:
        from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFileDialog
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, "Р’С‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ YARA-С„Р°Р№Р»", "",
            "YARA Rules (*.yar *.yara);;Р’СЃРµ С„Р°Р№Р»С‹ (*)"
        )
        if path:
            self._yara_path.setText(path)

    def _load_yara(self) -> None:
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
            cfg = Config()
            val = getattr(cfg, "yara_rules_path", None)
            if val:
                self._yara_path.setText(str(val))
            else:
                self._yara_path.clear()
        except ImportError:
            pass

    def _apply_yara(self) -> None:
        text = self._yara_path.text().strip()
        values = {"yara_rules_path": text if text else None}
        self.config_applied.emit(values)

        w = QWidget()

        form.setLabelAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft)

        self._ti_vt_key.setMaximumWidth(420)
        form.addRow("VirusTotal API key", self._ti_vt_key)

        self._ti_abusech_key = QLineEdit()

        self._ti_abusech_key.setPlaceholderText("ABUSECH_AUTH_KEY (MalwareBazaar, ThreatFox, URLhaus)")
        self._ti_abusech_key.setMaximumWidth(420)
        form.addRow("abuse.ch Auth-Key", self._ti_abusech_key)

        self._ti_greynoise_key.setPlaceholderText("GREYNOISE_API_KEY")
        self._ti_greynoise_key.setMaximumWidth(420)
        form.addRow("GreyNoise API key", self._ti_greynoise_key)

        self._ti_urlscan_key = QLineEdit()

        self._ti_urlscan_key.setPlaceholderText("URLSCAN_API_KEY")

            "</span>"

    def _toggle_api_keys_visibility(self, visible: bool) -> None:
        """РџРѕРєР°Р·Р°С‚СЊ/СЃРєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РєР»СЋС‡Рё РІ РѕР±РѕРёС… РїРѕР»СЏС…. РџРѕР»РµР·РЅРѕ, РєРѕРіРґР° РєРѕРїРёСЂСѓРµС€СЊ
        РґР»РёРЅРЅС‹Р№ РєР»СЋС‡ Рё С…РѕС‡РµС€СЊ СѓР±РµРґРёС‚СЊСЃСЏ, С‡С‚Рѕ РІСЃС‚Р°РІРёР»РѕСЃСЊ Р±РµР· РѕР±СЂРµР·РєРё.
        """
        mode = (
            QLineEdit.EchoMode.Normal if visible
            else QLineEdit.EchoMode.Password
        )
        self._ti_abuse_key.setEchoMode(mode)
        self._ti_vt_key.setEchoMode(mode)
        if hasattr(self, "_ti_abusech_key"):
            self._ti_abusech_key.setEchoMode(mode)

    def _load_threatintel(self) -> None:
        """Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚СЊ РїРѕР»СЏ РёР· Config Рё SecretsStore (keyring/env)."""
        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
        except ImportError:
            return
        cfg = Config()
        for key, widget in self._ti_inputs.items():
            value = getattr(cfg, key, None)
            if value is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(widget, QSpinBox):
                widget.setValue(int(value))
            elif isinstance(widget, QLineEdit):
                widget.setText(str(value))
            elif isinstance(widget, (QPlainTextEdit, EditableListWidget)):
                if isinstance(value, (frozenset, tuple, set, list)):
                    widget.setPlainText(_format_lines(value))
        # Р’СЃРµ РєР»СЋС‡Рё вЂ” С‡РµСЂРµР· SecretsStore: keyring -> session -> env.
        self._ti_abuse_key.setText(self._secrets.get("abuseipdb_api_key"))
        self._ti_vt_key.setText(self._secrets.get("virustotal_api_key"))
        self._ti_abusech_key.setText(self._secrets.get("abusech_auth_key"))
        self._ti_otx_key.setText(self._secrets.get("otx_api_key"))
        self._ti_greynoise_key.setText(self._secrets.get("greynoise_api_key"))
        self._ti_urlscan_key.setText(self._secrets.get("urlscan_api_key"))
    def _apply_threatintel(self) -> None:
        """РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РєР»СЋС‡Рё РІ keyring+env, РїРѕР»СЏ Config вЂ” РІ config_applied."""
        key_widgets = {
            "abuseipdb_api_key": self._ti_abuse_key,
            "virustotal_api_key": self._ti_vt_key,
            "abusech_auth_key": self._ti_abusech_key,
            "otx_api_key": self._ti_otx_key,
            "greynoise_api_key": self._ti_greynoise_key,
            "urlscan_api_key": self._ti_urlscan_key,
        }
        for name, widget in key_widgets.items():
            # SecretsStore.set РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµС‚ Рё keyring, Рё os.environ.
            self._secrets.set(name, widget.text())

        values: dict = {}
        for key, widget in self._ti_inputs.items():
            if isinstance(widget, QSpinBox):
                values[key] = widget.value()
            elif isinstance(widget, QLineEdit):
                if key == "enrichment_cache_path":
                    text = widget.text().strip()
                    if text:
                        try:
                            Path(text).parent.mkdir(
                                parents=True, exist_ok=True,
                            )
                        except OSError:
                            pass
                        values[key] = Path(text)
            elif isinstance(widget, QPlainTextEdit):
                values[key] = _parse_lines(widget.toPlainText())
        self.config_applied.emit(values)

    def _build_detectors_page(self) -> QWidget:
        w = QWidget()
        outer = QVBoxLayout(w)
        outer.setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24)
        outer.setSpacing(12)

        t, s = self._make_header(
            "Р”РµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂС‹",
            "РЎРЅСЏС‚Р°СЏ РіР°Р»РѕС‡РєР° РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡Р°РµС‚ РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ РІ СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµРј РїСЂРѕРіРѕРЅРµ. "
            "РџРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє СЃРѕРІРїР°РґР°РµС‚ СЃ СЏРґСЂРѕРј.",
        )
        outer.addWidget(t)
        outer.addWidget(s)

        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setFrameShape(QScrollArea.Shape.NoFrame)

        box_host = QWidget()
        box_layout = QVBoxLayout(box_host)
        box_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 8, 0, 8)
        box_layout.setSpacing(4)

        self._detector_boxes: dict[str, object] = {}
        for name, label in _DETECTORS:
            box_layout.addWidget(self._make_detector_row(name, label))
        box_layout.addStretch(1)

        scroll.setWidget(box_host)
        outer.addWidget(scroll, stretch=1)

        buttons = QHBoxLayout()
        all_btn = QPushButton("Р’РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ РІСЃРµ")
        all_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self._set_all_detectors(True))
        none_btn = QPushButton("РћС‚РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ РІСЃРµ")
        none_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self._set_all_detectors(False))
        buttons.addWidget(all_btn)
        buttons.addWidget(none_btn)
        buttons.addStretch(1)
        apply_btn = QPushButton("РџСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ")
        apply_btn.setObjectName("primary")
        apply_btn.clicked.connect(self._apply_detectors)
        buttons.addWidget(apply_btn)
        outer.addLayout(buttons)

        return w

    def _make_detector_row(self, name: str, label: str) -> QWidget:
        """РЎС‚СЂРѕРєР° РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂР°: [С‡РµРєР±РѕРєСЃ СЃ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµРј] [РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ СЃРїСЂР°РІР°]."""
        row = QWidget()
        h = QHBoxLayout(row)
        h.setContentsMargins(0, 2, 0, 2)
        h.setSpacing(12)

        cb = QCheckBox(label)
        cb.setChecked(True)
        cb.setMinimumWidth(220)
        h.addWidget(cb)

        help_text = get_detector_help(name)
        hint = QLabel(help_text)
        hint.setWordWrap(True)
        hint.setStyleSheet("color: #8a8a9a; font-size: 11px;")
        hint.setToolTip(help_text)
        h.addWidget(hint, stretch=1)

        self._detector_boxes[name] = cb
        return row

    def _set_all_detectors(self, checked: bool) -> None:
        for cb in self._detector_boxes.values():
            cb.setChecked(checked)

    def _apply_detectors(self) -> None:
        disabled = {
            name for name, cb in self._detector_boxes.items()
            if not cb.isChecked()
        }
        self.detectors_applied.emit(disabled)

    def _make_contradiction_row(self, name: str, label: str) -> QWidget:

    # =========================================================================
    # PERSISTENCE (settings_store)
    # =========================================================================

    def load_from_store(self) -> None:
        """РќР°РєР°С‚РёС‚СЊ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅРЅС‹Рµ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё РїРѕРІРµСЂС… РґРµС„РѕР»С‚РѕРІ РїСЂРё СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Рµ."""
        if self._store is None:
            return
        data = self._store.load()
        overrides = data.get("overrides", {})
        if not isinstance(overrides, dict):
            overrides = {}

        try:
            from paradox.config import Config
            defaults = Config()
        except ImportError:
            defaults = None

        for group in (
            self._threshold_inputs,
            self._brute_inputs,
            self._network_inputs,
            self._perf_inputs,
            self._allowlist_inputs,
            self._dga_inputs,
            self._reports_inputs,
            self._ti_inputs,
        ):
            for key, widget in group.items():
                if key not in overrides:
                    continue
                if defaults is not None:
                    default = getattr(defaults, key, None)
                    value = rehydrate(default, overrides[key]) if default is not None else overrides[key]
                else:
                    value = overrides[key]
                self._set_widget_value(widget, value, key)

        disabled_det = set(data.get("disabled_detectors", []) or [])
        for name, cb in self._detector_boxes.items():
            cb.setChecked(name not in disabled_det)

        disabled_contra = set(data.get("disabled_contradictions", []) or [])
        for name, cb in self._contradiction_boxes.items():
            cb.setChecked(name not in disabled_contra)

    def _save_overrides(self, values: dict) -> None:
        """Р”РѕРїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ overrides РІ store. РњРµСЂРґР¶ СЃ С‚РµРј, С‡С‚Рѕ СѓР¶Рµ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРѕ."""
        if self._store is None:
            return
        current = self._store.load()
        merged = dict(current.get("overrides", {}))
        merged.update(values)
        self._store.save_partial(overrides=merged)

    def _save_disabled_detectors(self, disabled) -> None:
        if self._store is None:
            return
        self._store.save_partial(disabled_detectors=set(disabled))

    def _save_disabled_contradictions(self, disabled) -> None:
        if self._store is None:
            return
        self._store.save_partial(disabled_contradictions=set(disabled))

    def _set_widget_value(self, widget, value, key: str) -> None:
        """Р—Р°РїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ РІ РІРёРґР¶РµС‚ СЃ СѓС‡С‘С‚РѕРј РµРіРѕ С‚РёРїР° Рё СЃРµРјР°РЅС‚РёРєРё РїРѕР»СЏ."""
        if isinstance(widget, QSpinBox):
            widget.setValue(int(value))
        elif isinstance(widget, QDoubleSpinBox):
            widget.setValue(float(value))
        elif isinstance(widget, QCheckBox):
            widget.setChecked(bool(value))
        elif isinstance(widget, QLineEdit):
            widget.setText(str(value))
        elif isinstance(widget, QPlainTextEdit):
            if key == "private_networks" and isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
                widget.setPlainText("\n".join(str(x) for x in value))
            elif _is_port_key(key) and isinstance(value, (set, frozenset, list, tuple)):
                widget.setPlainText(_format_ports(value))
            elif isinstance(value, (set, frozenset, list, tuple)):
                widget.setPlainText(_format_lines(value))
            else:
                widget.setPlainText(str(value))


_PORT_TEXT_KEYS = {
    "suspicious_ports",
    "port_scan_excluded_ports",
    "dc_service_ports",
    "lateral_movement_ports",
    "beaconing_mail_polling_ports",
    "brute_force_skip_tls_ports",
}


def _is_port_key(key: str) -> bool:
    if key in _PORT_TEXT_KEYS:
        return True
    return "ports" in key



# ==========================================

"""Р’РєР»Р°РґРєР° Kill-Chain РўР°Р№РјР»Р°Р№РЅ (РЈРјРЅС‹Р№ РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРі v4)."""
import re
from datetime import datetime as dt
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QTreeWidget, QTreeWidgetItem, 
    QHeaderView, QLabel, QSplitter, QTextBrowser, QLineEdit, QPushButton
)
from PySide6.QtGui import QColor, QFont, QBrush
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt

# РџР°С‚С‚РµСЂРЅ РґР»СЏ РїРѕРёСЃРєР° СЃРІСЏР·Рё "IP -> IP" (СЃ СѓС‡РµС‚РѕРј РІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅС‹С… РїСЂРѕР±РµР»РѕРІ РІРѕРєСЂСѓРі СЃС‚СЂРµР»РєРё)
ARROW_REGEX = re.compile(r'((?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3})\s*->\s*((?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3})')
# РџСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ РїРѕРёСЃРє IPv4 (РґР»СЏ fallback)
IP_REGEX = re.compile(r'\b(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}\b')

class TimelineView(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)
        layout.setSpacing(10)

        header_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.lbl_info = QLabel("РҐСЂРѕРЅРѕР»РѕРіРёСЏ РёРЅС†РёРґРµРЅС‚Р° РїРѕ С„Р°Р·Р°Рј Kill-Chain")
        self.lbl_info.setStyleSheet("font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; color: #e0e0e0;")
        header_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_info)
        header_layout.addStretch()

        self.search_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.search_edit.setPlaceholderText("РџРѕРёСЃРє РїРѕ IP, С‚РёРїСѓ РёР»Рё MITRE...")
        self.search_edit.setMinimumWidth(300)
        self.search_edit.textChanged.connect(self._apply_filter)
        header_layout.addWidget(self.search_edit)

        self.btn_expand = QPushButton("Р Р°Р·РІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ РІСЃРµ")
        self.btn_expand.clicked.connect(lambda: self.tree.expandAll())
        header_layout.addWidget(self.btn_expand)

        self.btn_collapse = QPushButton("РЎРІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ РІСЃРµ")
        self.btn_collapse.clicked.connect(lambda: self.tree.collapseAll())
        header_layout.addWidget(self.btn_collapse)

        layout.addLayout(header_layout)

        splitter = QSplitter(Qt.Orientation.Horizontal)
        layout.addWidget(splitter)

        self.tree = QTreeWidget()
        self.tree.setHeaderLabels([
            "РЎРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµ", "Р’СЂРµРјСЏ", "РСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє", "РќР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ", "Р”РµС‚Р°Р»Рё", "Conf", "MITRE"
        ])
        self.tree.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        self.tree.setSortingEnabled(False)

        header = self.tree.header()
        header.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(4, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(5, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(6, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)

        self.tree.itemClicked.connect(self._on_item_clicked)
        splitter.addWidget(self.tree)

        self.details = QTextBrowser()
        self.details.setStyleSheet("background: #2a2a35; border: 1px solid #444; border-radius: 4px;")
        self.details.setHtml("<p style='color:#888;'>РљР»РёРєРЅРёС‚Рµ РЅР° СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ СѓРІРёРґРµС‚СЊ РґРµС‚Р°Р»Рё.</p>")
        self.details.setMinimumWidth(350)
        splitter.addWidget(self.details)

        splitter.setSizes([850, 350])

    def _extract_ips(self, text: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
        """РР·РІР»РµРєР°РµС‚ Source Рё Destination РёР· С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°."""
        # 1. РџС‹С‚Р°РµРјСЃСЏ РЅР°Р№С‚Рё С‡РµС‚РєРёР№ РїР°С‚С‚РµСЂРЅ "IP -> IP"
        match = ARROW_REGEX.search(text)
        if match:
            return match.group(1), match.group(2)

        # 2. Р•СЃР»Рё РїР°С‚С‚РµСЂРЅР° РЅРµС‚, Р±РµСЂРµРј РїРµСЂРІС‹Рµ РґРІР° РЈРќРРљРђР›Р¬РќР«РҐ IP РІ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Рµ
        ips = IP_REGEX.findall(text)
        unique_ips = []
        for ip in ips:
            if ip not in unique_ips:
                unique_ips.append(ip)

        if len(unique_ips) >= 2:
            return unique_ips[0], unique_ips[1]
        elif len(unique_ips) == 1:
            return unique_ips[0], "вЂ”"
        return "вЂ”", "вЂ”"

    def update_data(self, report: dict):
        self.tree.clear()
        timeline = report.get("timeline", [])
        if not timeline:
            self.lbl_info.setText("РҐСЂРѕРЅРѕР»РѕРіРёСЏ РёРЅС†РёРґРµРЅС‚Р° (СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ)")
            return

        self.lbl_info.setText(f"РҐСЂРѕРЅРѕР»РѕРіРёСЏ РёРЅС†РёРґРµРЅС‚Р° (РЎРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№: {len(timeline)})")

        phases = {}
        for e in timeline:
            phase = e.get("phase", "Other")
            phases.setdefault(phase, []).append(e)

        sorted_phases = sorted(
            phases.keys(), 
            key=lambda p: min((e.get("chain_order", 999) for e in phases[p]))
        )

        t0 = min(e.get("ts", 0.0) for e in timeline)

        for phase_name in sorted_phases:
            events = phases[phase_name]

            phase_item = QTreeWidgetItem(self.tree)
            phase_item.setText(0, f"рџ“Ѓ {phase_name.upper()} ({len(events)})")
            font = QFont()
            font.setBold(True)
            phase_item.setFont(0, font)

            phase_colors = {
                "reconnaissance": "#44aaff",
                "execution": "#ffaa00",
                "persistence": "#ff8888",
                "lateral_movement": "#ff4444",
                "exfiltration": "#ff00ff",
                "impact": "#ff0000"
            }
            color = QColor(phase_colors.get(phase_name.lower(), "#888888"))
            phase_item.setForeground(0, QBrush(color))

            for e in events:
                child = QTreeWidgetItem(phase_item)

                ts = e.get("ts", 0.0)
                rel = ts - t0
                h = int(rel // 3600)
                m = int((rel % 3600) // 60)
                s = int(rel % 60)
                child.setText(1, f"{h}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}" if h > 0 else f"{m}:{s:02d}")

                kind = e.get("kind", "?")
                etype = e.get("type", "?")
                child.setText(0, f"{etype} ({kind})")

                # РџС‹С‚Р°РµРјСЃСЏ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РёС‚СЊ РїРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёСЏ
                full_text = f"{e.get('subject', '')} {e.get('description', '')} {e.get('value', '')}"
                src_ip, dst_ip = self._extract_ips(full_text)

                child.setText(2, src_ip)
                child.setText(3, dst_ip)

                # Р”РµС‚Р°Р»Рё
                desc = str(e.get("description") or "")
                child.setText(4, desc)

                conf = e.get("confidence")
                if isinstance(conf, (int, float)):
                    child.setText(5, f"{conf:.2f}")
                    if conf >= 0.85:
                        child.setForeground(5, QBrush(QColor("#ff4444")))
                    elif conf >= 0.7:
                        child.setForeground(5, QBrush(QColor("#ffaa00")))
                else:
                    child.setText(5, "вЂ”")

                child.setText(6, str(e.get("mitre_id") or "вЂ”"))

                child.setData(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, e)

        self.tree.expandAll()

    def _on_item_clicked(self, item: QTreeWidgetItem, column: int):
        data = item.data(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
        if not data:
            self.details.setHtml("<p style='color:#888;'>Р’С‹Р±СЂР°РЅР° С„Р°Р·Р°. РљР»РёРєРЅРёС‚Рµ РЅР° РєРѕРЅРєСЂРµС‚РЅРѕРµ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµ.</p>")
            return

        html = f"<h2 style='color:#44aaff; margin-bottom:5px;'>{data.get('type', '?')}</h2>"
        html += f"<p><b>Р¤Р°Р·Р°:</b> {data.get('phase', '?')}</p>"

        mitre = data.get('mitre_id')
        html += f"<p><b>MITRE:</b> {mitre if mitre else 'вЂ”'}</p>"

        conf = data.get("confidence", 0)
        conf_color = "#ff4444" if conf >= 0.85 else "#ffaa00" if conf >= 0.7 else "#44aaff"
        html += f"<p><b>Confidence:</b> <span style='color:{conf_color}; font-weight:bold;'>{conf:.2f}</span></p>"

        reasons = data.get("confidence_reasons") or []
        if reasons:
            html += "<h4 style='color:#aaa;'>РџРѕС‡РµРјСѓ РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ С‚Р°Рє СЂРµС€РёР»:</h4><ul>"
            for r in reasons:
                html += f"<li>{r}</li>"
            html += "</ul>"

        ev = data.get("evidence") or {}
        if ev:
            html += "<h4 style='color:#aaa;'>Р”РѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ (Evidence):</h4><ul>"
            for k, v in ev.items():
                html += f"<li><b>{k}:</b> {v}</li>"
            html += "</ul>"

        html += f"<hr><h4 style='color:#aaa;'>РџРѕР»РЅРѕРµ РѕРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ:</h4><p style='line-height:1.4;'>{data.get('description', '')}</p>"

        self.details.setHtml(html)

    def _apply_filter(self, text: str):
        text = text.lower()
        root = self.tree.invisibleRootItem()

        def filter_item(item):
            matches = False
            if not text:
                matches = True
            else:
                for col in range(self.tree.columnCount()):
                    if text in item.text(col).lower():
                        matches = True
                        break

            child_visible = False
            for i in range(item.childCount()):
                if filter_item(item.child(i)):
                    child_visible = True

            visible = matches or child_visible
            item.setHidden(not visible)

            if visible and item.childCount() > 0 and text:
                item.setExpanded(True)

            return visible

        for i in range(root.childCount()):
            filter_item(root.child(i))

"""РџР°РЅРµР»СЊ Р°РЅРѕРјР°Р»РёР№ (РѕР±РѕСЂРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё)."""
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QTableWidget, 
    QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView, QTabWidget, QWidget
)

class AbsencePanel(QFrame):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("card")
        self._build_ui()
        self.setVisible(False)

    def _build_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(16, 14, 16, 14)
        layout.setSpacing(10)

        title = QLabel("РђРЅРѕРјР°Р»РёРё / РћР±РѕСЂРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё")
        title.setObjectName("h1")
        layout.addWidget(title)

        self.tabs = QTabWidget()

        # Р’РєР»Р°РґРєР° 1: DNS Р±РµР· РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р°
        self.tab_dns = QWidget()
        vbox_dns = QVBoxLayout(self.tab_dns)
        vbox_dns.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.table_dns = QTableWidget(0, 2)
        self.table_dns.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Р”РѕРјРµРЅ (QNAME)", "РљРѕР»-РІРѕ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ"])
        self.table_dns.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.table_dns.setEditTriggers(QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
        vbox_dns.addWidget(self.table_dns)
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_dns, "DNS Р±РµР· РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р°")

        # Р’РєР»Р°РґРєР° 2: TCP Р±РµР· РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р°
        self.tab_tcp = QWidget()
        vbox_tcp = QVBoxLayout(self.tab_tcp)
        vbox_tcp.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.table_tcp = QTableWidget(0, 4)
        self.table_tcp.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Src IP", "Dst IP", "РџРѕСЂС‚", "РџР°РєРµС‚С‹"])
        self.table_tcp.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.table_tcp.setEditTriggers(QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
        vbox_tcp.addWidget(self.table_tcp)
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_tcp, "TCP Р±РµР· РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р° (SYN)")

        layout.addWidget(self.tabs)

    def update_report(self, report: dict):
        absence = report.get("absence", {})
        dns_data = absence.get("dns_unanswered", [])
        tcp_data = absence.get("tcp_unanswered", [])

        if not dns_data and not tcp_data:
            self.setVisible(False)
            return

        self.setVisible(True)

        # DNS
        self.table_dns.setRowCount(len(dns_data))
        for row, item in enumerate(dns_data):
            self.table_dns.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(str(item.get("qname", ""))))
            self.table_dns.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(str(item.get("count", ""))))

        # TCP
        self.table_tcp.setRowCount(len(tcp_data))
        for row, item in enumerate(tcp_data):
            self.table_tcp.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(str(item.get("src_ip", ""))))
            self.table_tcp.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(str(item.get("dst_ip", ""))))
            self.table_tcp.setItem(row, 2, QTableWidgetItem(str(item.get("dst_port", ""))))
            self.table_tcp.setItem(row, 3, QTableWidgetItem(str(item.get("packets", ""))))

        # РџРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµРј С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ С‚Сѓ РІРєР»Р°РґРєСѓ, РіРґРµ РµСЃС‚СЊ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ
        if not dns_data:
            self.tabs.setCurrentIndex(1)
        elif not tcp_data:
            self.tabs.setCurrentIndex(0)
